```typescript
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Sparkles,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  splitOptionString,
  getOptionMatchDetail,
  fetchNewCarCatalog,
  NewCarCatalogData,
  findOfficialCatalogOption,
  stripOptionPriceText
} from '@/services/biddingOptionCatalog';
import { OptionItem } from '@/services/chaolmaService';
import { copyToClipboard } from '@/utils/clipboard';
import { EncarItem } from './ComparableEncarSection';

interface ExtraOptionPriceResult {
  badgeText: string;
  tooltip: string;
}

/**
 * 추가옵션 총 가격 판정 (5단계 출처 순서)
 * 1순위: targetOptions 합계 (price_man 또는 price/10000)
 * 2순위 (신규): historyOptionTotalMan (차얼마 옵션 총액, 상단 신차대비와 동일 출처)
 * 3순위: optionsTag 안의 [옵션총액] 정규식
 * 4순위: 카탈로그 공식가 합계 (targetCatalog, 없으면 compCatalog 순)
 * 5순위: 위 모두 불가 시 '가격 미확인'
 */
function calculateExtraOptionsTotalPrice(
  targetOptions?: OptionItem[],
  optionsTag?: string,
  targetCatalog?: NewCarCatalogData | null,
  compCatalog?: NewCarCatalogData | null,
  historyOptionTotalMan?: number
): ExtraOptionPriceResult {
  // 1순위: targetOptions 합계가 0보다 크면 그대로 쓴다 (기존 차량번호 경로 동작 유지)
  if (targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0) {
    const sum1 = targetOptions.reduce((acc, c) => {
      const pMan = typeof c.price_man === 'number' && !isNaN(c.price_man) && c.price_man > 0
        ? c.price_man
        : (typeof c.price === 'number' && !isNaN(c.price) && c.price > 0 ? Math.round(c.price / 10000) : 0);
      return acc + pMan;
    }, 0);

    if (sum1 > 0) {
      const lines = targetOptions.map(c => {
        const pMan = (typeof c.price_man === 'number' && !isNaN(c.price_man) && c.price_man > 0)
          ? c.price_man
          : (typeof c.price === 'number' && !isNaN(c.price) && c.price > 0 ? Math.round(c.price / 10000) : 0);
        return `- ${c.name}: ${pMan > 0 ? `${pMan.toLocaleString()}만` : '가격미확인'}`;
      });
      return {
        badgeText: `총 ${sum1.toLocaleString()}만`,
        tooltip: `[옵션 상세 가격 (실차 데이터)]\n${lines.join('\n')}\n합계: ${sum1.toLocaleString()}만원`
      };
    }
  }

  // 2순위 (신규): 1순위가 0이거나 비어 있고, historyOptionTotalMan이 0보다 크면 총 {historyOptionTotalMan}만을 표시한다.
  // 단, 옵션이 하나도 없을 때(targetOptions도 비어 있고 optionsTag도 비어 있거나 옵션 없음이면)는 이 순위를 쓰지 않고 가격 미확인으로 떨어진다.
  const hasOptions = (targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0) || (optionsTag && optionsTag.trim() && !optionsTag.includes('옵션 없음'));
  if (hasOptions && typeof historyOptionTotalMan === 'number' && historyOptionTotalMan > 0) {
    return {
      badgeText: `총 ${historyOptionTotalMan.toLocaleString()}만`,
      tooltip: `[차얼마 옵션 총액 (상단 신차대비와 동일 출처)] 합계: ${historyOptionTotalMan.toLocaleString()}만원`
    };
  }

  // 3순위: optionsTag 안의 [옵션총액] 정규식 (기존 그대로)
  if (optionsTag && optionsTag.trim()) {
    const match = optionsTag.match(/\[(?:순정)?옵션총액:?\s*([0-9,]+만?)\]/);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const rawVal = match[1].trim();
      const badgeText = rawVal.endsWith('만') ? `총 ${rawVal}` : `총 ${rawVal}만`;
      return {
        badgeText,
        tooltip: `옵션 정보에 기재된 총액: ${rawVal}`
      };
    }
  }

  // 4순위: 카탈로그 공식가 합계 (targetCatalog -> compCatalog)
  const optNames: string[] = (targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0)
    ? targetOptions.map(o => o.name)
    : splitOptionString(optionsTag || '');

  if (optNames.length > 0) {
    let catalogSumMan = 0;
    const matchedLines: string[] = [];
    const unpricedOptions: string[] = [];

    for (const name of optNames) {
      const clean = stripOptionPriceText(name);
      if (!clean) continue;
      const official = findOfficialCatalogOption(clean, targetCatalog) || findOfficialCatalogOption(clean, compCatalog);
      if (official && typeof official.price === 'number' && official.price > 0) {
        const man = Math.round(official.price / 10000);
        catalogSumMan += man;
        matchedLines.push(`- ${clean}: ${man.toLocaleString()}만 (${official.officialName})`);
      } else {
        unpricedOptions.push(clean);
      }
    }

    if (matchedLines.length > 0 && catalogSumMan > 0) {
      let tooltip = `[신차 카탈로그 공식가 합계: ${catalogSumMan.toLocaleString()}만]\n${matchedLines.join('\n')}`;
      if (unpricedOptions.length > 0) {
        tooltip += `\n\n(공식가 미확인 옵션: ${unpricedOptions.join(', ')})`;
      }
      return {
        badgeText: `총 ${catalogSumMan.toLocaleString()}만`,
        tooltip
      };
    }
  }

  // 5순위: 가격 미확인
  return {
    badgeText: '가격 미확인',
    tooltip: '가격 출처가 없습니다 (실제 신차가 미제공)'
  };
}

export interface CockpitSidebarProps {
  heydealerUrl: string;
  setHeydealerUrl: (url: string) => void;
  isAiEstimating: boolean;
  handleHeydealerAiEstimate: () => void;
  setIsQuickPasteOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  carNumber: string;
  setCarNumber: (num: string) => void;
  isSearchingCar: boolean;
  handleLookupCarNumber: (num?: string) => void;
  aiEstimateStep?: string | null;
  searchStatus: { type: 'error' | 'warning' | 'info' | 'success'; message: string } | null;
  setSearchStatus: (st: { type: 'error' | 'warning' | 'info' | 'success'; message: string } | null) => void;
  manufacturer: string;
  setManufacturer: (m: string) => void;
  carName: string;
  setCarName: (name: string) => void;
  detailModel: string;
  setDetailModel: (m: string) => void;
  yearModel: number;
  setYearModel: (yr: number) => void;
  mileageKm: number;
  setMileageKm: (km: number) => void;
  optionsTag: string;
  targetOptions?: OptionItem[];
  unclassifiedBalance?: number;
  selectedEncar: EncarItem | null;
  expectedSellPrice: number;
  setExpectedSellPrice: (p: number) => void;
  outerRepairCount: number;
  setOuterRepairCount: (cnt: number) => void;
  auctionType: string;
  setAuctionType: (t: string) => void;
  targetMargin: number;
  setTargetMargin: (m: number) => void;
  userBid: number;
  setUserBid: (bid: number) => void;
  safeBidCeiling: number;
  actualMargin: number;
  actualPurchaseFee: number;
  repairCostTotal: number;
  memo: string;
  setMemo: (memo: string) => void;
  savedSuccess: boolean;
  handleSaveToLedger: () => void;
  targetCatalog?: NewCarCatalogData | null;
  historyOptionTotalMan?: number;
}

export const CockpitSidebar: React.FC<CockpitSidebarProps> = ({
  heydealerUrl,
  setHeydealerUrl,
  isAiEstimating,
  handleHeydealerAiEstimate,
  setIsQuickPasteOpen,
  carNumber,
  setCarNumber,
  isSearchingCar,
  handleLookupCarNumber,
  aiEstimateStep,
  searchStatus,
  setSearchStatus,
  manufacturer,
  setManufacturer,
  carName,
  setCarName,
  detailModel,
  setDetailModel,
  yearModel,
  setYearModel,
  mileageKm,
  setMileageKm,
  optionsTag,
  targetOptions = [],
  unclassifiedBalance = 0,
  selectedEncar,
  expectedSellPrice,
  setExpectedSellPrice,
  outerRepairCount,
  setOuterRepairCount,
  auctionType,
  setAuctionType,
  targetMargin,
  setTargetMargin,
  userBid,
  setUserBid,
  safeBidCeiling,
  actualMargin,
  actualPurchaseFee,
  repairCostTotal,
  memo,
  setMemo,
  savedSuccess,
  handleSaveToLedger,
  targetCatalog: propTargetCatalog,
  historyOptionTotalMan = 0,
}) => {
  const [internalCatalog, setInternalCatalog] = useState<NewCarCatalogData | null>(null);
  const [compCatalog, setCompCatalog] = useState<NewCarCatalogData | null>(null);
  const targetCatalog = propTargetCatalog !== undefined && propTargetCatalog !== null ? propTargetCatalog : internalCatalog;

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (carName) {
      fetchNewCarCatalog(carName, yearModel ? String(yearModel) : undefined).then((cat) => {
        if (isMounted) setInternalCatalog(cat);
      });
    } else {
      setInternalCatalog(null);
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [carName, yearModel]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (selectedEncar?.carName) {
      fetchNewCarCatalog(selectedEncar.carName, selectedEncar.year ? String(selectedEncar.year) : undefined).then((cat) => {
        if (isMounted) setCompCatalog(cat);
      });
    } else {
      setCompCatalog(null);
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [selectedEncar?.carName, selectedEncar?.year]);

  return (
      <div className="w-full lg:w-[320px] xl:w-[340px] shrink-0 space-y-4 lg:sticky lg:top-4 self-start max-h-[calc(100vh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-zinc-700 pr-1">
        <div className="bg-[#0e0f13] border border-[#262833] rounded-xl p-4 shadow-xl space-y-4">
          
          {/* Logo */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <div className="text-base font-black text-blue-400 font-serif-display tracking-wider">
                J-PRO
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-300 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">
                AUTO VALUATION INTELLIGENCE
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 font-bold border border-blue-500/30">
              비딩 콕핏 v2
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* URL Input */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-200 block">매물 주소</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="매물 URL 또는 ID 입력"
              value={heydealerUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setHeydealerUrl(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono"
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleHeydealerAiEstimate}
                disabled={isAiEstimating}
                className="py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-600/40 to-[#cc9166]/50 hover:from-amber-600/60 hover:to-[#cc9166]/70 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white border border-[#cc9166]/60 transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
              >
                {isAiEstimating ? <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-amber-300" /> : <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" />}
                <span>{isAiEstimating ? '산출 중...' : 'AI 견적 산출'}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsQuickPasteOpen(true)}
                className="py-2 rounded-lg bg-[#1a1c24] hover:bg-[#252836] text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-emerald-300 hover:text-emerald-200 border border-emerald-500/40 transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
                title="헤이딜러/카카오톡 텍스트 복사 후 1초 자동 분석"
              >
                <span>📋 텍스트 분석</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Car Number Search (차얼마 & 엔카 통합 조회) */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-200 block">차량번호 (필수)</label>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">Enter로 조회</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-1.5">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={carNumber}
                onChange={(e) => setCarNumber(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') handleLookupCarNumber();
                }}
                placeholder="예: 37다1840, 240어8733"
                className="flex-1 bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono tracking-wider"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleLookupCarNumber()}
                disabled={isSearchingCar}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white transition shadow-sm cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
              >
                {isSearchingCar ? <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Search className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span>{isSearchingCar ? '조회중' : '조회'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>


            {aiEstimateStep && (
              <div className="text-xs text-amber-300 bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg animate-pulse font-medium">
                {aiEstimateStep}
              </div>
            )}
            {searchStatus && (
              <div
                className={`text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-1.5 shadow-sm font-medium ${
                  searchStatus.type === 'error'
                    ? 'text-rose-200 bg-rose-950/60 border-rose-500/50'
                    : searchStatus.type === 'warning'
                    ? 'text-amber-200 bg-amber-950/60 border-amber-500/50'
                    : searchStatus.type === 'info'
                    ? 'text-blue-200 bg-blue-950/60 border-blue-500/50'
                    : 'text-emerald-200 bg-emerald-950/60 border-emerald-500/50'
                }`}
              >
                <span>{searchStatus.message}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Form Fields */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-[#262833] text-xs">
            
            {/* Manufacturer */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 block mb-1">제조사/브랜드</label>
              <select
                value={manufacturer}
                onChange={(e) => setManufacturer(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-zinc-100 font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              >
                <option value="기아">기아</option>
                <option value="현대">현대</option>
                <option value="제네시스">제네시스</option>
                <option value="르노코리아">르노코리아</option>
                <option value="KG모빌리티">KG모빌리티</option>
                <option value="쉐보레">쉐보레</option>
                <option value="BMW">BMW / 수입</option>
                {!['기아', '현대', '제네시스', '르노코리아', 'KG모빌리티', '쉐보레', 'BMW'].includes(manufacturer) && manufacturer && (
                  <option value={manufacturer}>{manufacturer}</option>
                )}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Car Name */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 block mb-1">차량명</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                list="cockpit-car-names"
                value={carName}
                onChange={(e) => setCarName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="예: 올 뉴 카니발, 더 뉴 팰리세이드"
                className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-white font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
              <datalist id="cockpit-car-names">
                <option value="더 뉴 팰리세이드" />
                <option value="팰리세이드" />
                <option value="올 뉴 카니발" />
                <option value="더 뉴 카니발" />
                <option value="카니발 4세대" />
                <option value="쏘나타 (DN8)" />
                <option value="그랜저 IG" />
                <option value="캐스퍼" />
                <option value="K5 3세대" />
                <option value="제네시스 G80" />
                <option value="XM3" />
                <option value="토레스" />
              </datalist>
            </div>

            {/* Detail Model */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 block mb-1">세부모델</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                list="cockpit-sub-models"
                value={detailModel}
                onChange={(e) => setDetailModel(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="세부모델명 입력"
                className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-zinc-100 font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
              <datalist id="cockpit-sub-models">
                <option value="디젤 2.2 4WD 프레스티지 (7인승)" />
                <option value="가솔린 3.8 2WD 캘리그래피" />
                <option value="럭셔리" />
                <option value="프레스티지" />
                <option value="디젤 9인승 프레스티지" />
                <option value="1.6 가솔린 스마트" />
                <option value="1.0 터보 인스퍼레이션" />
                <option value="시그니처" />
                <option value="스마트" />
                <option value="모던" />
                <option value="인스퍼레이션" />
                <option value="노블레스" />
              </datalist>
            </div>

            {/* Year */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-[#14151b] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">연식 (0=전체)</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setYearModel(Math.max(0, yearModel - 1))}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >-</button>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={yearModel === 0 ? '' : yearModel}
                  onChange={(e) => setYearModel(Number(e.target.value) || 0)}
                  placeholder="0"
                  className="w-12 text-center bg-[#1e212b] border border-[#2a2d3d] focus:border-sky-400 rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-black text-white text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setYearModel(yearModel + 1)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >+</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Mileage */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-[#14151b] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">
                주행거리 <span className="text-amber-400 font-bold">(필수, km)</span>
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setMileageKm(Math.max(0, mileageKm - 1000))}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >-</button>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={mileageKm === 0 ? '' : mileageKm.toLocaleString()}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const raw = e.target.value.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
                    setMileageKm(raw ? parseInt(raw, 10) : 0);
                  }}
                  placeholder="0"
                  className="w-24 text-center bg-[#1e212b] border border-[#2a2d3d] focus:border-sky-400 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-black text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setMileageKm(mileageKm + 1000)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >+</button>
              </div>
            </div>



            {/* Options */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5">
                {/* 1. 왼쪽: 추가 옵션 라벨 + 신차 옵션 N개 배지 */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">추가 옵션</span>
                  {((targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0) || (optionsTag && optionsTag.trim() && !optionsTag.includes('옵션 없음'))) && (() => {
                    const optCount = targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0 ? targetOptions.length : splitOptionString(optionsTag).length;
                    return (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] px-1.5 py-1 rounded bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-300 font-bold border border-blue-500/30">
                        신차 옵션 {optCount}개
                      </span>
                    );
                  })()}
                </div>

                {/* 2. 가운데: 추가옵션 총 가격 배지 (1곳으로 일원화, flex flex-1 items-center justify-center) */}
                {((targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0) || (optionsTag && optionsTag.trim() && !optionsTag.includes('옵션 없음'))) && (() => {
                  const priceInfo = calculateExtraOptionsTotalPrice(targetOptions, optionsTag, targetCatalog, compCatalog, historyOptionTotalMan);
                  return (
                    <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center min-w-0">
                      <span
                        title={priceInfo.tooltip}
                        className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] px-1.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-200 font-bold border border-amber-500/40 cursor-help truncate max-w-full"
                      >
                        {priceInfo.badgeText}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })()}

                {/* 3. 오른쪽: 우세 또는 조회차량 순정 배지 */}
                <div className="flex items-center shrink-0">
                  {(() => {
                    if (!selectedEncar?.optionsText || !optionsTag || !optionsTag.trim()) {
                      return <span className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] px-1.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 font-bold border border-emerald-500/30">조회차량 순정</span>;
                    }
                    const compOpts = splitOptionString(selectedEncar.optionsText);
                    const tgtOpts = splitOptionString(optionsTag);
                    if (compOpts.length === 0 || tgtOpts.length === 0) {
                      return <span className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] px-1.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 font-bold border border-emerald-500/30">조회차량 순정</span>;
                    }
                    const matchDetails = tgtOpts.map(to => getOptionMatchDetail(to, compOpts, 'target_vs_comp', targetCatalog, compCatalog));
                    const winCount = matchDetails.filter(d => !d.isMatched).length;
                    const exactCount = matchDetails.filter(d => d.confidence === 'exact').length;
                    const fuzzyCount = matchDetails.filter(d => d.confidence === 'fuzzy').length;

                    if (winCount > 0) {
                      return (
                        <span
                          title={`비교 매물(${selectedEncar.carName}) 대비 내 차 우세/고유 옵션: ${winCount}개\n공식 일치: ${exactCount}개 / 추정 매칭: ${fuzzyCount}개`}
                          className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] px-1.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 font-bold border border-emerald-500 cursor-help"
                        >
                          🟢 우세 {winCount}개
                        </span>
                      );
                    }
                    return (
                      <span
                        title={`비교 매물(${selectedEncar.carName})과 핵심 옵션 일치 (공식 일치: ${exactCount}개, 추정: ${fuzzyCount}개)`}
                        className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] px-1.5 py-1 rounded bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-300 font-bold border border-sky-400/50 cursor-help"
                      >
                        🔵 공통 사양
                      </span>
                    );
                  })()}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 🎨 옵션 색상 범례 안내 (비교차 선택 시 자동 전환) */}
              {optionsTag && optionsTag.trim().length > 0 && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] text-zinc-400 bg-zinc-900/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-zinc-800">
                  <span className="text-zinc-500 font-medium">범례:</span>
                  {selectedEncar?.optionsText ? (
                    <>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-400 font-semibold" title="비교차에는 없고 내 차에만 있는 고유/우세 옵션">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400"></span>내차우세(+)
                      </span>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-400 font-semibold" title="공식 명칭 차이로 스펙 유사로 추정 매칭된 옵션">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400"></span>추정(~)
                      </span>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sky-400 font-semibold" title="비교차와 내 차 모두 동일하게 장착된 공통 옵션">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-sky-400"></span>공통(✓)
                      </span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-sky-300 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-sky-400"></span>장착 순정 옵션 (비교 매물 클릭 시 우세 대조)
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Parsed Option Chips (백엔드 일원화 OptionItem 배열 직접 렌더링 + 미분류 잔액 뱃지) */}
              {(targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0) || (optionsTag && optionsTag.trim().length > 0) ? (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 py-1">
                  {(() => {
                    const compOpts = (selectedEncar?.options && selectedEncar.options.length > 0)
                      ? selectedEncar.options.map((o: OptionItem) => o.name)
                      : splitOptionString(selectedEncar?.optionsText || '');
                    const hasComp = compOpts.length > 0;

                    if (targetOptions && targetOptions.length > 0) {
                      return (
                        <>
                          {targetOptions.map((opt, idx) => {
                            const pMan = opt.price_man || Math.round(opt.price / 10000);
                            const displayName = pMan > 0 ? `${opt.name}(${pMan}만)` : opt.name;
                            const detail = getOptionMatchDetail(opt.name, compOpts, 'target_vs_comp', targetCatalog, compCatalog);

                            if (hasComp && !detail.isMatched) {
                              return (
                                <span
                                  key={idx}
                                  title={detail.tooltipText || opt.desc || `${opt.name} (${pMan}만)`}
                                  className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-400 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(34,197,94,0.25)] cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                                >
                                  <span className="text-emerald-400 text-[10px] font-black">+</span>
                                  <span>{displayName}</span>
                                </span>
                              );
                            }

                            if (hasComp && detail.confidence === 'fuzzy') {
                              return (
                                <span
                                  key={idx}
                                  title={detail.tooltipText || opt.desc || `${opt.name} (${pMan}만)`}
                                  className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-500/60 text-amber-300 font-semibold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                                >
                                  <span className="text-amber-400 text-[10px] font-black">~</span>
                                  <span>{displayName}</span>
                                </span>
                              );
                            }

                            return (
                              <span
                                key={idx}
                                title={detail.tooltipText || opt.desc || `[${opt.name}]\n조회 차량의 장착 순정 옵션입니다.`}
                                className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-sky-500/20 border border-sky-400/60 text-sky-300 font-semibold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                              >
                                <span className="text-sky-400 text-[10px]">{hasComp ? '✓' : '•'}</span>
                                <span>{displayName}</span>
                              </span>
                            );
                          })}

                          {unclassifiedBalance > 0 && (
                            <span
                              title={`카탈로그 옵션가 합계와 출고가 차액 간의 미분류 잔액입니다. 특정 옵션에 임의 배정되지 않은 순수 잔액입니다.`}
                              className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-500/50 text-amber-300 font-medium text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help font-mono"
                            >
                              <span>ℹ️ 미분류 잔액 ({unclassifiedBalance}만)</span>
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </>
                      );
                    }

                    const tgtOpts = splitOptionString(optionsTag);
                    if (tgtOpts.length === 0) {
                      return (
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 py-1 italic">
                          추가 옵션 없음 (기본 출고 사양)
                        </div>
                      );
                    }

                    return tgtOpts.map((opt, idx) => {
                      const detail = getOptionMatchDetail(opt, compOpts, 'target_vs_comp', targetCatalog, compCatalog);

                      if (hasComp && !detail.isMatched) {
                        return (
                          <span
                            key={idx}
                            title={detail.tooltipText}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-400 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(34,197,94,0.25)] cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                          >
                            <span className="text-emerald-400 text-[10px] font-black">+</span>
                            <span>{opt}</span>
                          </span>
                        );
                      }

                      if (hasComp && detail.confidence === 'fuzzy') {
                        return (
                          <span
                            key={idx}
                            title={detail.tooltipText}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-500/60 text-amber-300 font-semibold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                          >
                            <span className="text-amber-400 text-[10px] font-black">~</span>
                            <span>{opt}</span>
                          </span>
                        );
                      }

                      return (
                        <span
                          key={idx}
                          title={detail.tooltipText || `[${opt}]\n조회 차량의 장착 순정 옵션입니다.`}
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-sky-500/20 border border-sky-400/60 text-sky-300 font-semibold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                        >
                          <span className="text-sky-400 text-[10px]">{hasComp ? '✓' : '•'}</span>
                          <span>{opt}</span>
                        </span>
                      );
                    });
                  })()}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 py-1 italic">
                  추가 옵션 없음 (기본 출고 사양)
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Expected Sell Price */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-[#14151b] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">예상 소매가 (만원)</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setExpectedSellPrice(Math.max(0, expectedSellPrice - 10))}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >-</button>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={expectedSellPrice === 0 ? '' : expectedSellPrice}
                  onChange={(e) => setExpectedSellPrice(Number(e.target.value) || 0)}
                  placeholder="0"
                  className="w-16 text-center bg-[#1e212b] border border-[#2a2d3d] focus:border-sky-400 rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-black text-sky-400 text-sm sm:text-base font-mono focus:outline-none"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setExpectedSellPrice(expectedSellPrice + 10)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >+</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Outer Repairs */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-[#14151b] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">외판 수리 갯수</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setOuterRepairCount(Math.max(0, outerRepairCount - 1))}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >-</button>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={outerRepairCount}
                  onChange={(e) => setOuterRepairCount(Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value) || 0))}
                  min={0}
                  className="w-12 text-center bg-[#1e212b] border border-[#2a2d3d] focus:border-sky-400 rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-black text-white text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setOuterRepairCount(outerRepairCount + 1)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >+</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Auction Type */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 block mb-1">매입 경로</label>
              <select
                value={auctionType}
                onChange={(e) => setAuctionType(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-zinc-200 font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm"
              >
                <option value="셀프(기본)">셀프(기본)</option>
                <option value="제로">제로</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Target Margin */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-[#14151b] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">목표 마진 (만원)</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTargetMargin(Math.max(0, targetMargin - 10))}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >-</button>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={targetMargin === 0 ? '' : targetMargin}
                  onChange={(e) => setTargetMargin(Number(e.target.value) || 0)}
                  placeholder="0"
                  className="w-14 text-center bg-[#1e212b] border border-[#2a2d3d] focus:border-emerald-400 rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-black text-emerald-400 text-sm sm:text-base font-mono focus:outline-none"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTargetMargin(targetMargin + 10)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-[#232634] hover:bg-[#323646] text-white font-bold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center text-xs"
                >+</button>
              </div>
            </div>



            {/* 🎯 가로 1행: [입찰가 수정 입력창] + [📋 복사 버튼] */}
            {expectedSellPrice > 0 && (
              <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 block mb-1">최종 입찰가 (만원)</label>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      value={userBid}
                      onChange={(e) => setUserBid(Number(e.target.value) || 0)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-emerald-500/60 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-emerald-400 font-black text-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-400 font-mono"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={async () => {
                      const success = await copyToClipboard(String(userBid));
                      if (success) {
                        setSearchStatus({
                          type: 'success',
                          message: `📋 입찰가 ${userBid.toLocaleString()}만원 클립보드 복사 완료!`
                        });
                      } else {
                        setSearchStatus({
                          type: 'error',
                          message: `❌ 클립보드 복사 실패: 브라우저 권한을 확인해주세요.`
                        });
                      }
                      setTimeout(() => setSearchStatus(null), 2500);
                    }}
                    className="self-end px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-[#1f222e] hover:bg-[#2c3040] text-zinc-200 hover:text-white border border-[#35394a] text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"
                    title="클립보드에 복사"
                  >
                    <span>📋 복사</span>
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* 🏷️ 가로 2행: 권장매입가 및 실시간 마진 노출 (녹색 창 클릭 시 장부 즉시 저장) */}
                <div 
                  onClick={handleSaveToLedger}
                  className="bg-emerald-500/15 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 border border-emerald-500/40 hover:border-emerald-400/70 p-3.5 rounded-xl space-y-2 transition cursor-pointer shadow-md group"
                  title="클릭 시 내 장부에 즉시 저장됩니다"
                >
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-zinc-200">
                      {userBid !== safeBidCeiling ? (
                        <span>최종 매입가 <small className="text-zinc-400 font-normal">(권장 {safeBidCeiling.toLocaleString()}만)</small></span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-zinc-200">권장 매입가</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-emerald-400 tracking-tight font-mono">
                      {userBid.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-300">만원</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-2 border-t border-dashed border-emerald-500/30 text-xs sm:text-sm">
                    <span className="text-zinc-300 font-semibold">예상마진</span>
                    <span className="text-blue-400 font-black text-base font-mono">+{Math.round(actualMargin).toLocaleString()}만원</span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[11px] text-zinc-400 pt-0.5">
                    <span>수수료: {actualPurchaseFee}만 · 수리: {repairCostTotal}만 · 잡비: 15만</span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-300 font-bold group-hover:underline">💾 클릭 저장</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Memo */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 block mb-1">특이사항 / 메모</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="특이사항 메모 (보조키, 틴팅, 블박 등)"
                value={memo}
                onChange={(e) => setMemo(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-[#14151b] border border-[#2c2f3d] rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-zinc-200 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Save to Ledger Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSaveToLedger}
              className={`w-full py-3 rounded-lg text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-md cursor-pointer ${
                savedSuccess
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-[#1f222e] hover:bg-[#2c3040] text-white border border-[#35394a]'
              }`}
            >
              {savedSuccess ? '✓ 저장 완료!' : '💾 내 장부 및 구글시트에 저장'}
            </button>

          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
  );
};
```

// FILE PATH: frontend\src\components\BiddingCockpit\Sections\ComparableEncarSection.tsx (Lines: 1614)

```typescript
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, ExternalLink, Car } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  splitOptionString,
  getOptionMatchDetail,
  fetchNewCarCatalog,
  NewCarCatalogData
} from '@/services/biddingOptionCatalog';
import { OptionItem } from '@/services/chaolmaService';
import { compareEncarItemsByBackendRule } from '@/services/cockpitService';
import { CarDamageDiagram2D, getDamagePartKoreanName } from './CarDamageDiagram2D';
import { TrackedCarsModal } from './TrackedCarsModal';
import { KcarMarketStats, KcarCarItem, KcarDetailData } from '../types';
import { getCarColorStyle } from '@/utils/carColor';

export interface EncarItem {
  id: string;
  carName: string;
  subModel?: string;
  modelName?: string;
  year: number | string;
  mileage: number;
  price: number;
  accidentType: string;
  color?: string;
  checkDate?: string;
  holdingDays?: number | null;
  optionsText?: string;
  options?: OptionItem[];
  originPrice?: number;
  optionPrice?: number;
  newCarPrice?: number;
  [key: string]: any;
}

export type EncarSortOption =
  | 'price_asc'
  | 'price_desc'
  | 'recent_reg'
  | 'year_desc'
  | 'mileage_asc'
  | 'complex';

export type KcarSortOption =
  | 'price_asc'
  | 'price_desc'
  | 'year_desc'
  | 'mileage_asc';

export interface EncarFilterStats {
  targetBadge?: string;
  totalModelCount?: number;
  excludedByBadge?: number;
  validCount?: number;
  filterMethod?: string;
  reason?: string;
}

export interface ComparableEncarSectionProps {
  encarList: EncarItem[];
  encarTotalModelCount: number;
  encarFilteredCount: number;
  encarFilterStats?: EncarFilterStats | null;
  selectedEncarId: string;
  selectedEncar: EncarItem | null;
  isEncarLoading: boolean;
  isInspectionLoading: boolean;
  encarErrorMessage?: string;
  encarSourceUrl?: string;
  carName?: string;
  carNumber?: string;
  detailModel?: string;
  yearModel: number;
  mileageKm: number;
  optionsTag?: string;
  showTrimColumn?: boolean;
  onToggleTrimColumn?: () => void;
  onSelectEncar: (id: string) => void;
  kcarStats?: KcarMarketStats | null;
}

export const ComparableEncarSection: React.FC<ComparableEncarSectionProps> = ({
  encarList,
  encarTotalModelCount,
  encarFilteredCount,
  encarFilterStats,
  selectedEncarId,
  selectedEncar,
  isEncarLoading,
  isInspectionLoading,
  encarErrorMessage,
  encarSourceUrl,
  carName,
  carNumber,
  detailModel,
  yearModel,
  mileageKm,
  optionsTag,
  showTrimColumn: externalShowTrimColumn,
  onToggleTrimColumn,
  onSelectEncar,
  kcarStats,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'encar' | 'kcar'>('encar');
  const [internalShowTrim, setInternalShowTrim] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const showTrimColumn = externalShowTrimColumn !== undefined ? externalShowTrimColumn : internalShowTrim;
  const [sortOption, setSortOption] = useState<EncarSortOption>('complex');
  const [kcarSortOption, setKcarSortOption] = useState<KcarSortOption>('price_asc');

  // 자사 차량 매핑 맵 연동 (백엔드 캐시와 동기화하여 100% 자사 매물 식별)
  const [companyCarIdMap, setCompanyCarIdMap] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [trackedCarIds, setTrackedCarIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [isTrackedModalOpen, setIsTrackedModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const fetchTrackedCarIds = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/tracking/cars');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success && Array.isArray(data.items)) {
        setTrackedCarIds(new Set(data.items.map((i: any) => String(i.carid))));
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTrackedCarIds();
  }, []);

  const handleToggleTrack = async (car: EncarItem, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const cid = String(car.id || car._carid || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!cid) return;

    if (trackedCarIds.has(cid)) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/tracking/cars/${cid}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
          setTrackedCarIds(prev => {
            const next = new Set(prev);
            next.delete(cid);
            return next;
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    } else {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/tracking/cars', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            carid: cid,
            carName: car.carName || '',
            price: Number(car.price) || 0,
            source: 'cockpit',
          })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
          setTrackedCarIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(cid));
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/autoplus/company_carids')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
          setCompanyCarIdMap(data);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const isCompanyCarItem = (car: EncarItem): boolean => {
    if (car.isCompanyCar) return true;
    const cleanId = String(car.id || car._carid || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (cleanId && companyCarIdMap[cleanId]) return true;
    return false;
  };

  const companyCount = useMemo(() => {
    return (encarList || []).filter(isCompanyCarItem).length;
  }, [encarList, companyCarIdMap]);

  const sortedEncarList = useMemo(() => {
    if (!encarList || encarList.length === 0) return [];
    const list = [...encarList];

    const parseYearNum = (y: any): number => {
      if (typeof y === 'number') return y < 100 ? 2000 + y : y;
      const str = String(y || '').trim();
      const m = str.match(/(?:20)?(\d{2})/);
      return m ? Number('20' + m[1]) : 0;
    };

    const getValidDateStr = (item: EncarItem): string => {
      const d = item.checkDate || item.registrationDate || item.regDate || '';
      return String(d).replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    };

    switch (sortOption) {
      case 'price_asc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0));
      case 'price_desc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => (Number(b.price) || 0) - (Number(a.price) || 0));
      case 'recent_reg':
        return list.sort((a, b) => {
          const dateA = getValidDateStr(a);
          const dateB = getValidDateStr(b);
          if (dateA !== dateB) return dateB.localeCompare(dateA);
          return (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0);
        });
      case 'year_desc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => {
          const yrDiff = parseYearNum(b.year) - parseYearNum(a.year);
          if (yrDiff !== 0) return yrDiff;
          return (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0);
        });
      case 'mileage_asc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => {
          const milA = Number(a.mileage ?? a.km ?? 0);
          const milB = Number(b.mileage ?? b.km ?? 0);
          if (milA !== milB) return milA - milB;
          return (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0);
        });
      case 'complex':
        // 백엔드 DataProcessor.sort_by_perf_and_year와 1:1 완벽 일치하는 단일 복합 정렬 (Phase 3):
        // 1순위: 점검 완료 우선 -> 2순위: 성능점검일 최신순 -> 3순위: 가격 낮은순 -> 4순위: 연식 최신순
        return list.sort(compareEncarItemsByBackendRule);
      default:
        return list;
    }
  }, [encarList, sortOption]);

  const kcarCars: KcarCarItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (kcarStats?.wideCars && kcarStats.wideCars.length > 0) {
      return kcarStats.wideCars;
    }
    return kcarStats?.cars || [];
  }, [kcarStats]);

  const [selectedKcarCarCd, setSelectedKcarCarCd] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [kcarDetail, setKcarDetail] = useState<KcarDetailData | null>(null);
  const [isKcarDetailLoading, setIsKcarDetailLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [kcarInspectionMap, setKcarInspectionMap] = useState<Record<string, KcarDetailData>>({});
  const kcarDetailCacheRef = useRef<Record<string, KcarDetailData>>({});

  // 🚀 [케이카 사전 프리패치(Prefetch)]: 케이카 목록이 준비되면 성능점검일·재고일을 100ms 간격 순차 로드 (3D 도면은 상세 클릭 시로 지연)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!kcarCars || kcarCars.length === 0) return;

    let isCancelled = false;
    const prefetchList = kcarCars.slice(0, 20);

    const runPrefetch = async () => {
      for (const car of prefetchList) {
        if (isCancelled) break;
        const cd = car.carCd;
        if (!cd) continue;

        if (kcarDetailCacheRef.current[cd]) continue;

        try {
          const res = await fetch('/api/kcar/detail', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ carCd: cd, include3d: false })
          });
          if (isCancelled) break;
          const json = await res.json();
          if (json.success && json.data && !isCancelled) {
            kcarDetailCacheRef.current[cd] = json.data;
            setKcarInspectionMap(prev => ({
              ...prev,
              [cd]: json.data
            }));
          }
        } catch (e) {
          // 백그라운드 프리패치는 무음 처리
        }

        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));
      }
    };

    runPrefetch();

    return () => {
      isCancelled = true;
    };
  }, [kcarCars]);

  const sortedKcarList = useMemo(() => {
    const list = kcarCars.map(car => {
      const det = kcarInspectionMap[car.carCd];
      if (!det) return car;
      let checkDate = det.checkDate && det.checkDate !== '-' ? det.checkDate : car.checkDate;
      let holdingDays = typeof det.holdingDays === 'number' && !isNaN(det.holdingDays) && det.holdingDays >= 0
        ? det.holdingDays
        : car.holdingDays;
      const pfmncRaw = det.dates?.pfmncDate || '';
      const pDigits = pfmncRaw.replace(/\D/g, '');
      if ((!checkDate || checkDate === '-') && pDigits.length >= 8) {
        checkDate = `${pDigits.slice(2, 4)}-${pDigits.slice(4, 6)}-${pDigits.slice(6, 8)}`;
      }
      return {
        ...car,
        checkDate: checkDate || '-',
        holdingDays: holdingDays !== undefined ? holdingDays : null,
      };
    });
    const parseYearNum = (val: any): number => {
      if (!val) return 0;
      const str = String(val);
      const m = str.match(/(?:20)?(\d{2})/);
      return m ? Number('20' + m[1]) : 0;
    };

    switch (kcarSortOption) {
      case 'price_asc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0));
      case 'price_desc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => (Number(b.price) || 0) - (Number(a.price) || 0));
      case 'year_desc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => {
          const yrDiff = parseYearNum(b.year || b.fullYear) - parseYearNum(a.year || a.fullYear);
          if (yrDiff !== 0) return yrDiff;
          return (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0);
        });
      case 'mileage_asc':
        return list.sort((a, b) => {
          const milA = Number(a.mileage ?? 0);
          const milB = Number(b.mileage ?? 0);
          if (milA !== milB) return milA - milB;
          return (Number(a.price) || 0) - (Number(b.price) || 0);
        });
      default:
        return list;
    }
  }, [kcarCars, kcarInspectionMap, kcarSortOption]);

  // 케이카 목록 변경 시 유효 엄격 차량 중에서만 첫 번째 차량 자동 선택 (없으면 선택 없음)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'kcar' && sortedKcarList.length > 0) {
      if (!selectedKcarCarCd || !sortedKcarList.some(c => c.carCd === selectedKcarCarCd)) {
        const strictMatch = sortedKcarList.find(c => c.isStrict);
        setSelectedKcarCarCd(strictMatch ? strictMatch.carCd : null);
      }
    }
  }, [activeTab, sortedKcarList, selectedKcarCarCd]);


  const selectedKcar = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedKcarCarCd) return null;
    return sortedKcarList.find(c => c.carCd === selectedKcarCarCd) || null;
  }, [sortedKcarList, selectedKcarCarCd]);

  const [kcarDetailError, setKcarDetailError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // 케이카 단일 차량 상세 정보 API 호출 (상세 카드용: include3d=true, 7초 타임아웃 + 실패 안내)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedKcarCarCd) {
      setKcarDetail(null);
      setKcarDetailError(null);
      return;
    }

    const cached = kcarDetailCacheRef.current[selectedKcarCarCd];
    if (cached && cached.has3d) {
      setKcarDetail(cached);
      setKcarDetailError(null);
      setIsKcarDetailLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    if (cached) {
      setKcarDetail(cached);
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      controller.abort();
    }, 7000); // 7초 타임아웃

    setIsKcarDetailLoading(true);
    setKcarDetailError(null);

    fetch('/api/kcar/detail', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ carCd: selectedKcarCarCd, include3d: true }),
      signal: controller.signal
    })
      .then(res => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        return res.json();
      })
      .then(json => {
        if (json.success && json.data) {
          kcarDetailCacheRef.current[selectedKcarCarCd] = json.data;
          setKcarInspectionMap(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            [selectedKcarCarCd]: json.data
          }));
          setKcarDetail(json.data);
          setKcarDetailError(null);
        } else {
          if (!cached) setKcarDetail(null);
          setKcarDetailError(json.message || '상세 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
        }
      })
      .catch(err => {
        if (err.name === 'AbortError') {
          console.warn(`[K-Car Detail] carCd=${selectedKcarCarCd} 타임아웃 (7초 초과)`);
          setKcarDetailError('상세 정보 요청 시간이 초과되었습니다.');
        } else {
          console.error('[K-Car Detail Fetch Error]', err);
          setKcarDetailError('상세 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
        }
        if (!cached) setKcarDetail(null);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        setIsKcarDetailLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [selectedKcarCarCd]);

  const [targetCatalog, setTargetCatalog] = useState<NewCarCatalogData | null>(null);
  const [compCatalog, setCompCatalog] = useState<NewCarCatalogData | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (carName) {
      fetchNewCarCatalog(carName, yearModel ? String(yearModel) : undefined).then((cat) => {
        if (isMounted) setTargetCatalog(cat);
      });
    } else {
      setTargetCatalog(null);
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [carName, yearModel]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (selectedEncar?.carName) {
      fetchNewCarCatalog(selectedEncar.carName, selectedEncar.year ? String(selectedEncar.year) : undefined).then((cat) => {
        if (isMounted) setCompCatalog(cat);
      });
    } else {
      setCompCatalog(null);
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [selectedEncar?.carName, selectedEncar?.year]);

  const [kcarCompCatalog, setKcarCompCatalog] = useState<NewCarCatalogData | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    const model = kcarDetail?.modelName || selectedKcar?.carName || selectedKcar?.modelName;
    const yr = kcarDetail?.year || selectedKcar?.year || selectedKcar?.fullYear;
    if (model) {
      fetchNewCarCatalog(model, yr ? String(yr) : undefined).then((cat) => {
        if (isMounted) setKcarCompCatalog(cat);
      });
    } else {
      setKcarCompCatalog(null);
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [kcarDetail?.modelName, selectedKcar?.carName, selectedKcar?.modelName, kcarDetail?.year, selectedKcar?.year, selectedKcar?.fullYear]);
  const toggleTrim = onToggleTrimColumn || (() => setInternalShowTrim(prev => !prev));

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#0e0f13] border-2 border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      {/* 비교 매물 영역 상단 명확한 구분 배너 & 탭 전환 */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 border-b border-[#262833] bg-amber-500/5 -mx-4 -mt-4 p-4 rounded-t-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500/25 text-amber-300 font-black text-xs border border-amber-500/40 shadow-sm">
            🔍 시장 비교 매물 영역
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#14151b] p-0.5 rounded-lg border border-[#2b2e3d]">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('encar')}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-bold transition cursor-pointer ${
                activeTab === 'encar'
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-black shadow-sm font-black'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#1f222e]'
              }`}
            >
              엔카 ({encarTotalModelCount || encarList.length}대)
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('kcar')}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-bold transition cursor-pointer ${
                activeTab === 'kcar'
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-black shadow-sm font-black'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#1f222e]'
              }`}
            >
              케이카 ({kcarCars.length}대)
            </button>
          </div>
          <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm sm:text-base ml-1">
            {activeTab === 'encar'
              ? `엔카 실시간 등록 매물 리스트 (${encarTotalModelCount || encarList.length}대)`
              : `케이카 실시간 등록 매물 리스트 (${kcarCars.length}대)`}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
          ⚠️ 아래 목록은 시장 시세 비교군이며, <strong className="text-sky-300">{carNumber || carName || '타겟차량'}</strong>의 성능/점검 정보가 아닙니다.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 탭 분기: 엔카 (테이블 7칸 + 상세카드 5칸) vs 케이카 (테이블 7칸 + 상세카드 5칸) */}
      {activeTab === 'encar' && (
        /* Encar List + 2D Detail Layout */
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-12 gap-4 items-stretch">
        {/* Table (Left 7 cols) */}
        <div className="xl:col-span-7 space-y-2 flex flex-col">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white px-1">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <span>📰 엔카 동급 매물 (전체 {encarTotalModelCount}대 · 시세 계산 표본 {encarFilteredCount}대)</span>
              {companyCount > 0 && (
                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-black bg-indigo-500/30 text-indigo-200 border border-indigo-400/50">
                  자사 {companyCount}대
                </span>
              )}
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-medium hidden sm:inline whitespace-nowrap">정렬:</span>
                <select
                  value={sortOption}
                  onChange={(e) => setSortOption(e.target.value as EncarSortOption)}
                  className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1c1e28] hover:bg-[#282c3c] text-zinc-200 border border-[#35394a] transition cursor-pointer font-bold focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400"
                >
                  <option value="price_asc">가격 낮은 순</option>
                  <option value="price_desc">가격 높은 순</option>
                  <option value="recent_reg">최신 등록순</option>
                  <option value="year_desc">연식 최신순</option>
                  <option value="mileage_asc">주행거리 적은 순</option>
                  <option value="complex">복합 정렬</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={toggleTrim}
                className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1c1e28] hover:bg-[#282c3c] text-zinc-200 border border-[#35394a] transition cursor-pointer font-bold"
              >
                <span>세부등급 {showTrimColumn ? '숨김' : '표시'}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsTrackedModalOpen(true)}
                className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 transition cursor-pointer font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
                title="관심 등록된 매물 목록 및 팔리기 직전 호가 확인"
              >
                <span>🎯 관심 추적</span>
                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-amber-400 text-black text-[10px] font-black font-mono">
                  {trackedCarIds.size}
                </span>
              </button>
              <span className="text-xs text-amber-300/90 font-medium hidden sm:inline">👆 행 클릭 시 해당 비교 매물 점검표 열림</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* [필터링 원칙 준수 안내 배너: 기준 등급 일치 매물만 포함, 제외 대수 및 사유 실시간 표시] */}
          {encarFilterStats ? (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/10 via-indigo-500/10 to-transparent border border-amber-500/30 rounded-lg text-xs">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 font-extrabold border border-amber-500/40">
                  🎯 기준 등급: {encarFilterStats.targetBadge || detailModel || '동급'}
                </span>
                <span className="text-zinc-300 text-xs">
                  전체 <strong className="text-white font-mono">{encarFilterStats.totalModelCount || encarTotalModelCount}대</strong> 중
                  {encarFilterStats.excludedByBadge && encarFilterStats.excludedByBadge > 0 ? (
                    <> <strong className="text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold">엄격 기준 불일치로 {encarFilterStats.excludedByBadge.toLocaleString()}대 제외</strong> ➔</>
                  ) : (
                    <> 기준 등급 100% 일치 ➔</>
                  )}
                  {" "}최종 유효 표본 <strong className="text-emerald-400 font-mono font-bold">{encarFilterStats.validCount ?? encarFilteredCount}대</strong>
                </span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">
                ⚡ {encarFilterStats.filterMethod === 'API' ? '엔카 API 배지 필터 적용' : '로컬 엄격 일치 필터 적용'}
              </span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            encarTotalModelCount > encarFilteredCount && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-lg text-xs text-amber-300">
                <span>💡 필터링 기준: 엄격 기준 불일치로 {(encarTotalModelCount - encarFilteredCount).toLocaleString()}대 제외 ➔ 유효 {encarFilteredCount}대 표본 채택</span>
              </div>
            )
          )}


          <div className="overflow-x-auto h-[560px] max-h-[560px] overflow-y-auto border border-[#262833] rounded-xl scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-zinc-700 bg-[#0a0b0e] flex-1">
            {isEncarLoading ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-3 py-12 bg-[#0e0f13]/80">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 border-3 border-blue-500/30 border-t-blue-400 rounded-full animate-spin" />
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span>🚗 엔카 실시간 동급 매물 정밀 크롤링 중...</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                  성능점검 기록부 및 추가옵션 전수 스캔 &amp; 2D 도면 연동 중
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : encarList.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-3 py-16 px-4 bg-[#0a0b0e] text-center">
                {encarErrorMessage ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 flex items-center justify-center text-rose-400">
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-bold text-rose-300">
                      {encarErrorMessage}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-[340px] leading-relaxed">
                      엔카 실시간 매물 조회 중 오류가 발생했거나 통신이 원활하지 않습니다.
                    </div>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-zinc-800/40 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-400">
                      <Car className="w-6 h-6" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-300">
                      해당 조건의 엔카 동급 매물이 없습니다.
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-[340px] leading-relaxed">
                      현재 조건(차종/연식/주행거리)에 일치하는 시장 등록 매물이 없습니다.
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}
                {encarSourceUrl && (
                  <a
                    href={encarSourceUrl}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blue-600/20 hover:bg-blue-600/30 text-blue-300 border border-blue-500/30 text-xs font-semibold transition mt-2 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <span>엔카 웹사이트에서 직접 조회하기</span>
                    <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <table className="w-full text-left text-[12px] text-zinc-200">
                <thead className="bg-[#14151b] sticky top-0 z-10 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 font-bold uppercase border-b border-[#262833]">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center w-7 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap" title="관심 추적">추적</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center w-7 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap">선택</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[72px]">성능일</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[52px]">재고일</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1.5 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[82px]">차량명</th>
                    {showTrimColumn && <th className="py-2 px-1.5 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[95px]">세부등급</th>}
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[58px]">연식</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1.5 text-right bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[84px]">주행(km)</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1.5 text-right bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[78px]">💰 가격</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-2 text-left bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[125px] min-w-[125px]">사고유무</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[50px]">색상</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[72px]">옵션</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#232634]">
                  {sortedEncarList.map((car) => {
                    const isSelected = car.id === selectedEncarId;
                    const targetYrStr = String(yearModel % 100).padStart(2, '0');
                    const regYrMatch = String(car.year || '').match(/^\s*(\d{2})/);
                    const isTargetYear = regYrMatch ? regYrMatch[1] === targetYrStr : false;
                    const parsedCarOpts = splitOptionString(car.optionsText || '');
                    const hasAddedOptions = parsedCarOpts.length > 0;

                    // 사고유무 5~6글자 간결 규격화 (왼쪽 정렬용)
                    const formatAccident = (rawType: string) => {
                      if (!rawType || (rawType.includes('완전무사고') && !rawType.includes('교환') && !rawType.includes('판금'))) {
                        return { text: '🟢 완전무사고', style: 'text-emerald-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      if (rawType.includes('사고') && !rawType.includes('무사고')) {
                        return { text: '🔴 프레임사고', style: 'text-rose-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      const repMatch = rawType.match(/교환\s*[:\s]?\s*(\d+)/);
                      const fixMatch = rawType.match(/판금\s*[:\s]?\s*(\d+)/);
                      const rCnt = repMatch ? parseInt(repMatch[1], 10) : 0;
                      const fCnt = fixMatch ? parseInt(fixMatch[1], 10) : 0;
                      const total = rCnt + fCnt;
                      if (total > 0) {
                        return { text: `🟡 단순수리 (${total})`, style: 'text-amber-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      if (rawType.includes('교환') && rawType.includes('판금')) {
                        return { text: '🟡 단순(교/판)', style: 'text-amber-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      if (rawType.includes('판금')) {
                        return { text: '🟡 단순판금', style: 'text-amber-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      if (rawType.includes('교환')) {
                        return { text: '🟡 단순교환', style: 'text-amber-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      return { text: `🟡 ${rawType.slice(0, 6)}`, style: 'text-amber-400 font-bold' };
                    };
                    const accInfo = formatAccident(car.accidentType);
                    const isCompany = isCompanyCarItem(car);

                    return (
                      <tr
                        key={car.id}
                        onClick={() => onSelectEncar(car.id)}
                        title={isCompany ? "자사(오토플러스/리본카) 보유 광고 매물" : undefined}
                        className={`cursor-pointer transition ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'bg-blue-600/30 text-white font-bold'
                            : isCompany
                              ? 'bg-indigo-900/60 hover:bg-indigo-900/75 text-indigo-100'
                              : 'hover:bg-[#14151c]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={(e) => handleToggleTrack(car, e)}
                            className={`p-0.5 rounded transition hover:scale-125 text-sm ${
                              trackedCarIds.has(String(car.id))
                                ? 'text-amber-400 font-bold drop-shadow-[0_0_4px_rgba(251,191,36,0.5)]'
                                : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-amber-300'
                            }`}
                            title={trackedCarIds.has(String(car.id)) ? "관심 추적 중 (클릭 시 해제)" : "관심 추적 등록 (엔카 판매종료 시 호가 확정 추적)"}
                          >
                            {trackedCarIds.has(String(car.id)) ? '★' : '☆'}
                          </button>
                        </td>
                        <td className={`py-1.5 px-1 text-center whitespace-nowrap ${
                          isCompany ? 'border-l-4 border-indigo-400' : ''
                        }`}>
                          <input
                            type="radio"
                            name="encarSelect"
                            checked={isSelected}
                            onChange={() => onSelectEncar(car.id)}
                            className="accent-blue-500 cursor-pointer w-3.5 h-3.5"
                          />
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[12px] text-zinc-100 font-mono font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.checkDate || '-'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.holdingDays !== undefined && car.holdingDays !== null ? (
                            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-black font-mono border ${
                              car.holdingDays <= 14 
                                ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40' 
                                : car.holdingDays >= 60 
                                  ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/40' 
                                  : 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                            }`}>
                              {car.holdingDays}일
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-zinc-500 font-mono text-[11px]">-</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            {isCompany && (
                              <span
                                className="inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black bg-indigo-500/30 text-indigo-200 border border-indigo-400/50 shadow-sm shrink-0"
                                title="자사(오토플러스/리본카) 보유 및 엔카 광고 등록 매물"
                              >
                                자사
                              </span>
                            )}
                            <a
                              href={`https://fem.encar.com/cars/detail/${String(car.id).replace(/\D/g, '')}`}
                              target="_blank"
                              rel="noopener noreferrer"
                              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                              className={`hover:underline font-bold text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap transition-colors ${
                                isCompany ? 'text-indigo-300 hover:text-indigo-200' : 'text-sky-400 hover:text-sky-300'
                              }`}
                              title={isCompany ? `자사 매물 엔카 바로가기 (매물코드: ${car.id})` : `엔카 공식 상세페이지 새창 열기 (매물코드: ${car.id})`}
                            >
                              {car.modelName || car.carName}
                            </a>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        {showTrimColumn && (
                          <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
                            {car.subModel || '-'}
                          </td>
                        )}
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap font-mono">
                          <span className={isTargetYear ? 'text-sky-400 font-black' : 'text-zinc-300 font-semibold'}>
                            {((car as any).regYear ? String((car as any).regYear).slice(-2) : (regYrMatch ? regYrMatch[1] : car.year))}


                          </span>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 text-right text-[12px] text-white font-bold font-mono whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div>{car.mileage.toLocaleString()}</div>
                          {mileageKm > 0 && (() => {
                            const diffKm = car.mileage - mileageKm;
                            if (diffKm === 0) return <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-mono">동일km</div>;
                            return (
                              <div className={`text-[10px] font-mono leading-none pt-0.5 ${diffKm > 0 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}`}>
                                {diffKm > 0 ? `+${diffKm.toLocaleString()}km` : `${diffKm.toLocaleString()}km`}
                              </div>
                            );
                          })()}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 text-right font-black text-amber-400 font-mono text-[13px] whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.price.toLocaleString()}만
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-2 text-left whitespace-nowrap" title={car.accidentType}>
                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11.5px] ${accInfo.style}`}>
                            {accInfo.text}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[11px] text-zinc-300 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.color || '흰색'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                          {hasAddedOptions ? (() => {
                            const targetOpts = splitOptionString(optionsTag || '');
                            const compDetails = parsedCarOpts.map(o => getOptionMatchDetail(o, targetOpts, 'comp_vs_target', targetCatalog, compCatalog));
                            const exactCount = compDetails.filter(d => d.confidence === 'exact').length;
```
