```typescript
                            const fuzzyCount = compDetails.filter(d => d.confidence === 'fuzzy').length;
                            const commonCount = compDetails.filter(d => d.isMatched).length;
                            const extraCount = parsedCarOpts.length - commonCount;

                            const tooltipLines = [
                              `[비교 차량 추가옵션 총 ${parsedCarOpts.length}개]`,
                              ...compDetails.map((d, i) => {
                                const o = parsedCarOpts[i];
                                if (d.confidence === 'exact') return `✓ ${o} (신차 공식 일치: ${d.matchedOppositeName})`;
                                if (d.confidence === 'fuzzy') return `~ ${o} (퍼지 추정 매칭: ${d.matchedOppositeName})`;
                                return `+ ${o} (비교차 전용 옵션)`;
                              }),
                              '──────────────────────────────',
                              targetOpts.length > 0
                                ? `공식 일치: ${exactCount}개 / 추정 매칭: ${fuzzyCount}개 / 비교차 전용: ${extraCount}개`
                                : '내 차 옵션 정보 없음'
                            ];

                            return (
                              <span 
                                title={tooltipLines.join('\n')}
                                className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 font-bold border border-amber-500/40 text-[10.5px] cursor-help hover:bg-amber-500/30 transition inline-flex items-center gap-0.5"
                              >
                                {exactCount > 0 ? (
                                  <span className="text-sky-400 text-[10px]">✓</span>
                                ) : fuzzyCount > 0 ? (
                                  <span className="text-amber-400 text-[10px]">~</span>
                                ) : null}
                                <span>{parsedCarOpts.length}개</span>
                              </span>
                            );
                          })() : (
                            <span title="기본 출고 사양 (추가옵션 없음)" className="text-zinc-500 text-[10.5px] cursor-help">기본</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right: Detailed Inspection Card + 2D Car Diagram (Right 5 cols) - 비교 매물 선택 시에만 표출 */}
        <div className="xl:col-span-5 bg-[#14151b] border border-[#262833] rounded-xl p-4 space-y-3 self-start flex flex-col justify-start">
          {!selectedEncar ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-center space-y-3 py-16 px-4 bg-[#0a0b0e] rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[#2a2d3d]">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400">
                <Car className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white">비교 매물이 선택되지 않았습니다</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-[280px] leading-relaxed">
                좌측 <span className="text-amber-300 font-semibold">엔카 비교 매물 목록</span>에서 확인하고 싶은 차량의 행을 클릭하면, 해당 비교 매물의 <span className="text-sky-300 font-semibold">외판/골격 2D 상태도와 성능점검 기록부</span>가 여기에 표시됩니다.
              </div>
              {carNumber && (
                <div className="mt-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#161822] border border-[#282a3a] text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                  현재 조회 차량: <strong className="text-emerald-400 font-mono">{carNumber}</strong> ({carName} / {mileageKm.toLocaleString()}km)
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              {/* 1. Header Title & Grade (줄 블록: 제목과 등급만 포함, 케이카와 1:1 대칭) */}
              <div className="space-y-2 border-b border-zinc-600 pb-2.5">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                      <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-white">{selectedEncar.carName}</span>
                      {isCompanyCarItem(selectedEncar) && (
                        <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-indigo-500/30 text-indigo-200 font-black text-[10px] border border-indigo-400/50">
                          🏢 자사 매물
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {isInspectionLoading && (
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-amber-400 font-mono animate-pulse">
                          ⚡ 점검표 수신 중...
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-300 font-semibold">{selectedEncar.subModel || detailModel}</div>
                  </div>
                  {/* 선택한 차량의 신차가(추가옵션값) 표기 */}
                  {Boolean(selectedEncar.newCarPrice || selectedEncar.originPrice) && (
                    <span
                      className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-amber-300 font-mono shrink-0 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30"
                      title={
                        typeof selectedEncar.optionPrice === 'number'
                          ? `엔카 신차가: ${((selectedEncar.newCarPrice || selectedEncar.originPrice) || 0).toLocaleString()}만원 (출고 등급가: ${(selectedEncar.originPrice || 0).toLocaleString()}만 + 선택옵션: ${selectedEncar.optionPrice.toLocaleString()}만)`
                          : `엔카 신차가: ${((selectedEncar.newCarPrice || selectedEncar.originPrice) || 0).toLocaleString()}만원`
                      }
                    >
                      {((selectedEncar.newCarPrice || selectedEncar.originPrice) || 0).toLocaleString()}만
                      {typeof selectedEncar.optionPrice === 'number' && (
                        <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200/90 ml-1">
                          ({selectedEncar.optionPrice.toLocaleString()}만)
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 2. 칩스: 연식 / 주행 km / 색상 / 점검일 / 사고 배지 (줄 블록 밖으로 분리) */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-0.5 text-xs">
                  <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1f222e] text-white font-bold font-mono">
                    {selectedEncar.year}년식
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1f222e] text-emerald-400 font-bold font-mono">
                    {selectedEncar.mileage.toLocaleString()}km
                  </span>
                  {(() => {
                    const colorStyle = getCarColorStyle(selectedEncar.color);
                    return (
                      <span
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-medium border"
                        style={{
                          backgroundColor: colorStyle.bgColor,
                          color: colorStyle.textColor,
                          borderColor: colorStyle.borderColor
                        }}
                      >
                        {colorStyle.dot} {colorStyle.displayName}
                      </span>
                    );
                  })()}
                  {selectedEncar.inspectionDate && selectedEncar.inspectionDate !== '-' ? (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 font-bold font-mono" title="법정 성능점검일자">
                      📋 점검: {selectedEncar.inspectionDate}{typeof selectedEncar.holdingDays === 'number' && !isNaN(selectedEncar.holdingDays) && selectedEncar.holdingDays >= 0 ? ` (${selectedEncar.holdingDays}일 전)` : ''}
                    </span>
                  ) : selectedEncar.checkDate && selectedEncar.checkDate !== '-' ? (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 font-bold font-mono" title="법정 성능점검일자">
                      📋 점검: {selectedEncar.checkDate}{typeof selectedEncar.holdingDays === 'number' && !isNaN(selectedEncar.holdingDays) && selectedEncar.holdingDays >= 0 ? ` (${selectedEncar.holdingDays}일 전)` : ''}
                    </span>
                  ) : null}
                  {selectedEncar.accidentType.includes('완전무사고') && !selectedEncar.accidentType.includes('교환') && !selectedEncar.accidentType.includes('판금') ? (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 font-bold border border-emerald-500/40">
                      🟢 {selectedEncar.accidentType}
                    </span>
                  ) : selectedEncar.accidentType.includes('사고') && !selectedEncar.accidentType.includes('무사고') ? (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 font-bold border border-rose-500/40">
                      🔴 {selectedEncar.accidentType}
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 font-bold border border-amber-500/40">
                      🟡 {selectedEncar.accidentType}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* 사고 내역 텍스트 (스크롤 없이 한눈에 확인하도록 배지 바로 아래로 이동) */}
                {(() => {
                  const repList = ((selectedEncar?.replaceNames && selectedEncar.replaceNames.length > 0) ? selectedEncar.replaceNames : selectedEncar?.replaces) || [];
                  const fixList = ((selectedEncar?.repairNames && selectedEncar.repairNames.length > 0) ? selectedEncar.repairNames : selectedEncar?.repairs) || [];
                  const mappedRepList = repList.map(getDamagePartKoreanName);
                  const mappedFixList = fixList.map(getDamagePartKoreanName);
                  const hasRep = mappedRepList.length > 0;
                  const hasFix = mappedFixList.length > 0;

                  if (!hasRep && !hasFix) return null;

                  return (
                    <div className="pt-1.5 pb-0.5 text-xs flex flex-col gap-1">
                      {hasRep && (
                        <div className="text-rose-400 font-medium flex items-start gap-1">
                          <span className="font-bold shrink-0">🔴 교환(X):</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-rose-300">{mappedRepList.join(', ')}</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {hasFix && (
                        <div className="text-amber-400 font-medium flex items-start gap-1">
                          <span className="font-bold shrink-0">🟡 판금/용접(W):</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-amber-300">{mappedFixList.join(', ')}</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })()}

              {/* Large Price Display */}
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5 pt-1 pb-0.5">
                <span className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-amber-400 font-mono tracking-tight">
                  {selectedEncar.price.toLocaleString()}
                </span>
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-300">만원</span>
              </div>

              {/* Parsed Individual Option Badges */}
              {(() => {
                const optItems = (selectedEncar.options && selectedEncar.options.length > 0)
                  ? selectedEncar.options.map((o: OptionItem) => {
                      const pMan = o.price_man || Math.round(o.price / 10000);
                      return pMan > 0 ? `${o.name}(${pMan}만)` : o.name;
                    })
                  : splitOptionString(selectedEncar.optionsText || '');

                if (optItems.length === 0) {
                  return (
                    <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0c0e14] rounded-lg border border-[#232634] text-xs text-zinc-500 italic">
                      추가옵션 없음 또는 기본 트림 사양
                    </div>
                  );
                }

                const targetOpts = splitOptionString(optionsTag || '');

                const exactOpts: { name: string; tooltip: string; detail: any }[] = [];
                const fuzzyOpts: { name: string; tooltip: string; detail: any }[] = [];
                const extraOpts: { name: string; tooltip: string; detail: any }[] = [];

                for (const opt of optItems) {
                  const detail = getOptionMatchDetail(opt, targetOpts, 'comp_vs_target', targetCatalog, compCatalog);
                  if (detail.confidence === 'exact') {
                    exactOpts.push({ name: opt, tooltip: detail.tooltipText, detail });
                  } else if (detail.confidence === 'fuzzy') {
                    fuzzyOpts.push({ name: opt, tooltip: detail.tooltipText, detail });
                  } else {
                    extraOpts.push({ name: opt, tooltip: detail.tooltipText, detail });
                  }
                }

                // 공식 일치(하늘색 ✓) -> 퍼지 추정(주황색 ~) -> 비교차 전용(오렌지 +) 순 정렬
                const sortedOptItems = [
                  ...exactOpts,
                  ...fuzzyOpts,
                  ...extraOpts
                ];

                const displayOpts = sortedOptItems.slice(0, 8);
                const overflowCount = sortedOptItems.length - 8;

                return (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 p-2 bg-[#0c0e14] rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                    {displayOpts.map((item, idx) => {
                      if (item.detail.confidence === 'exact') {
                        return (
                          <span
                            key={idx}
                            title={item.tooltip}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-300 border border-sky-400 shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(14,165,233,0.2)] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition max-w-[210px]"
                          >
                            <span className="text-sky-400 font-black shrink-0">✓</span>
                            <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                          </span>
                        );
                      }
                      if (item.detail.confidence === 'fuzzy') {
                        return (
                          <span
                            key={idx}
                            title={item.tooltip}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/60 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition max-w-[210px]"
                          >
                            <span className="text-amber-400 font-black shrink-0">~</span>
                            <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                          </span>
                        );
                      }
                      return (
                        <span
                          key={idx}
                          title={item.tooltip}
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400 border border-orange-500/60 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition max-w-[210px]"
                        >
                          <span className="text-orange-400 font-black shrink-0">+</span>
                          <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                        </span>
                      );
                    })}
                    {overflowCount > 0 && (
                      <span
                        className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium bg-[#1e222d] text-slate-400 border border-[#2e384d] cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                        title={`추가 옵션:\n${sortedOptItems.slice(8).map(o => `- ${o.name}`).join('\n')}`}
                      >
                        +{overflowCount}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })()}

              {/* 2D Car Damage Diagram Component */}
              <CarDamageDiagram2D selectedEncar={selectedEncar} />
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      )}

      {/* 케이카 탭 (엔카와 완전 동일 7:5 양식: 좌측 7칸 테이블 + 우측 5칸 상세카드) */}
      {activeTab === 'kcar' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-12 gap-4 items-stretch">
          {/* Left: K-Car List (7 cols) */}
          <div className="xl:col-span-7 space-y-2 flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white px-1">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <span>📰 K-Car 직영 매물 (전체 {kcarCars.length}대 · 시세 계산 표본 {kcarStats?.strictCount ?? kcarStats?.count ?? 0}대)</span>
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-medium hidden sm:inline whitespace-nowrap">정렬:</span>
                  <select
                    value={kcarSortOption}
                    onChange={(e) => setKcarSortOption(e.target.value as KcarSortOption)}
                    className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1c1e28] hover:bg-[#282c3c] text-zinc-200 border border-[#35394a] transition cursor-pointer font-bold focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400"
                  >
                    <option value="price_asc">가격 낮은 순</option>
                    <option value="price_desc">가격 높은 순</option>
                    <option value="year_desc">연식 신순</option>
                    <option value="mileage_asc">주행거리 적은 순</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={toggleTrim}
                  className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1c1e28] hover:bg-[#282c3c] text-zinc-200 border border-[#35394a] transition cursor-pointer font-bold"
                >
                  <span>세부등급 {showTrimColumn ? '숨김' : '표시'}</span>
                </button>
                <span className="text-xs text-amber-300/90 font-medium hidden sm:inline">👆 행 클릭 시 해당 비교 매물 상세카드 열림</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* 헤드라인 통계 칩스 (총 대수, 평균, 주행보정평균, 최저, 중간, 최고) */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-2 text-center text-xs">
              <div className="bg-[#181a24] p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">무사고 평균가</div>
                <div className="font-mono font-bold text-rose-300 text-sm">
                  {kcarStats && kcarStats.avg > 0 ? `${kcarStats.avg.toLocaleString()}만` : '-'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-[#181a24] p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">주행보정 평균</div>
                <div className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 text-sm">
                  {kcarStats && kcarStats.avgAdj && kcarStats.avgAdj > 0 ? `${kcarStats.avgAdj.toLocaleString()}만` : '-'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-[#181a24] p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">⬇️ 최저가</div>
                <div className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-400 text-sm">
                  {kcarStats && kcarStats.min > 0 ? `${kcarStats.min.toLocaleString()}만` : '-'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-[#181a24] p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">🎯 중간값</div>
                <div className="font-mono font-bold text-zinc-200 text-sm">
                  {kcarStats && kcarStats.median > 0 ? `${kcarStats.median.toLocaleString()}만` : '-'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-[#181a24] p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">⬆️ 최고가</div>
                <div className="font-mono font-bold text-rose-400 text-sm">
                  {kcarStats && kcarStats.max > 0 ? `${kcarStats.max.toLocaleString()}만` : '-'}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* 케이카 테이블 (좌측 7칸, 엔카와 100% 동일 양식 및 가로스크롤 보장) */}
            <div className="overflow-x-auto max-h-[580px] rounded-lg border border-[#232634] bg-[#0c0d12]">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-[12px] text-zinc-200">
                <thead className="bg-[#14151b] sticky top-0 z-10 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 font-bold uppercase border-b border-[#262833]">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center w-7 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap">선택</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[72px]">성능일</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[52px]">재고일</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1.5 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[82px]">차량명</th>
                    {showTrimColumn && <th className="py-2 px-1.5 bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[95px]">세부등급</th>}
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[58px]">연식</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1.5 text-right bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[84px]">주행(km)</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1.5 text-right bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[78px]">💰 가격</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-2 text-left bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[115px] min-w-[115px]">사고유무</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[50px]">색상</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-1 text-center bg-[#14151b] whitespace-nowrap w-[72px]">지점</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#232634]">
                  {sortedKcarList.map((car, idx) => {
                    const isSelected = selectedKcarCarCd === car.carCd;
                    const targetYrStr = String(yearModel % 100).padStart(2, '0');
                    const regYrMatch = String(car.fullYear || car.year || '').match(/(?:20)?(\d{2})/);
                    const isTargetYear = regYrMatch ? regYrMatch[1] === targetYrStr : false;

                    const formatAccident = (rawType: string) => {
                      if (!rawType || rawType.includes('무사고')) {
                        return { text: '🟢 무사고', style: 'text-emerald-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      if (rawType.includes('단순수리')) {
                        return { text: '🟡 단순수리', style: 'text-amber-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      if (rawType.includes('사고')) {
                        return { text: '🔴 사고', style: 'text-rose-400 font-bold' };
                      }
                      return { text: rawType, style: 'text-zinc-300 font-medium' };
                    };
                    const accInfo = formatAccident(car.accident);

                    return (
                      <tr
                        key={car.carCd || idx}
                        onClick={() => setSelectedKcarCarCd(car.carCd)}
                        className={`cursor-pointer transition ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'bg-blue-600/30 text-white font-bold'
                            : 'hover:bg-[#14151c]'
                        }`}
                        title="클릭 시 우측 상세카드에 점검/옵션 정보가 표시됩니다."
                      >
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                          <input
                            type="radio"
                            name="kcarSelect"
                            checked={isSelected}
                            onChange={() => setSelectedKcarCarCd(car.carCd)}
                            className="accent-blue-500 cursor-pointer w-3.5 h-3.5"
                          />
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[12px] text-zinc-100 font-mono font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.checkDate || '-'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                          {typeof car.holdingDays === 'number' && !isNaN(car.holdingDays) && car.holdingDays >= 0 ? (
                            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-black font-mono border ${
                              car.holdingDays <= 14
                                ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                                : car.holdingDays >= 60
                                  ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/40'
                                  : 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                            }`}>
                              {car.holdingDays}일
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-zinc-500 font-mono text-[11px]">-</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 truncate max-w-[82px] font-medium" title={car.carName}>
                          <a
                            href={car.detailUrl || `https://www.kcar.com/bc/detail/carInfoDtl?i_sCarCd=${car.carCd}`}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                            className="hover:underline font-bold text-[12px] text-sky-400 hover:text-sky-300 whitespace-nowrap transition-colors"
                            title="새 탭에서 케이카 상세페이지 열기"
                          >
                            {car.modelName || car.carName}
                          </a>
                        </td>
                        {showTrimColumn && (
                          <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 truncate max-w-[95px] text-[11px] text-zinc-300 font-medium" title={car.gradeDetail || car.grade || ''}>
                            {car.gradeDetail || car.grade || '-'}
                          </td>
                        )}
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span className={isTargetYear ? 'text-amber-300 font-bold font-mono' : 'text-zinc-300 font-mono'}>
                            {(car.fullYear ? String(car.fullYear).slice(-2) : (car.year ? String(car.year).slice(-2) : '-'))}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 text-right text-[12px] font-mono text-zinc-200 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.mileage ? `${Number(car.mileage).toLocaleString()}km` : '-'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1.5 text-right font-bold text-amber-300 font-mono text-[13px] whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.price ? `${Number(car.price).toLocaleString()}만` : '-'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-2 text-left whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span className={`text-[11px] ${accInfo.style}`}>
                            {accInfo.text}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[11px] text-zinc-300 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {car.color || '-'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-1.5 px-1 text-center text-[11px] text-zinc-300 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-800/80 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-700/50">
                            {car.center || '-'}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                  {sortedKcarList.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={showTrimColumn ? 11 : 10}
                        className="py-12 text-center text-zinc-400 font-medium"
                      >
                        {kcarStats && !kcarStats.success
                          ? (kcarStats.message || 'K-Car 매물 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.')
                          : '해당 조건의 K-Car 직영 매물이 없습니다.'}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right: K-Car Detail Card (5 cols) - 엔카 1:1 동일 양식 */}
          <div className="xl:col-span-5 bg-[#14151f] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#262833] space-y-3 self-start flex flex-col justify-start">
            {!selectedKcar ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-center space-y-3 py-16 px-4 bg-[#0a0b0e] rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[#2a2d3d]">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400">
                  <Car className="w-6 h-6" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white">비교 매물이 선택되지 않았습니다</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-[280px] leading-relaxed">
                  좌측 <span className="text-amber-300 font-semibold">케이카 비교 매물 목록</span>에서 확인하고 싶은 차량의 행을 클릭하면, 해당 비교 매물의 <span className="text-orange-300 font-semibold">점검일자, 사고 배지, 추가옵션</span>이 여기에 표시됩니다.
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                {/* 1. Header Title & Link */}
                <div className="space-y-2 border-b border-[#232634] pb-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                      <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-white" title={selectedKcar.carName}>
                        {kcarDetail?.modelName || selectedKcar.modelName || selectedKcar.carName}
                      </span>
                      <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-300 font-extrabold text-[10px] border border-orange-500/40">
                        K-Car 직영
                      </span>
                      {isKcarDetailLoading && (
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-amber-400 font-mono animate-pulse">
                          ⚡ 상세 정보 수신 중...
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {/* 신차가는 케이카 원문 미제공이므로 미표시하고, JATO 추가 옵션 합계만 표시 */}
                      {kcarDetail?.options && kcarDetail.options.totalPrice > 0 && (
                        <span
                          className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-amber-300 font-mono shrink-0 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30"
                          title="케이카 JATO 추가 선택옵션 합계 (출고 등급 신차가는 원문 미제공)"
                        >
                          옵션합계 +{kcarDetail.options.totalPrice.toLocaleString()}만
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-300 font-semibold">
                    {kcarDetail?.gradeFullName || selectedKcar.gradeDetail || selectedKcar.grade || ''}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* 상세 수신 실패/타임아웃 안내 */}
                {kcarDetailError && !isKcarDetailLoading && (
                  <div className="p-2.5 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/30 rounded-lg text-rose-300 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span>⚠️ {kcarDetailError}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 2. 칩스: 연식 / 주행 km / 색상 / 점검일(있을 때만) / 사고 배지 */}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-0.5 text-xs">
                  <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1f222e] text-white font-bold font-mono">
                    {selectedKcar.fullYear || selectedKcar.year || kcarDetail?.year}년식
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#1f222e] text-emerald-400 font-bold font-mono">
                    {(kcarDetail?.mileage || selectedKcar.mileage || 0).toLocaleString()}km
                  </span>
                  {(() => {
                    const rawColor = kcarDetail?.color || selectedKcar.color;
                    const colorStyle = getCarColorStyle(rawColor);
                    return (
                      <span
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-medium border"
                        style={{
                          backgroundColor: colorStyle.bgColor,
                          color: colorStyle.textColor,
                          borderColor: colorStyle.borderColor
                        }}
                      >
                        {colorStyle.dot} {colorStyle.displayName}
                      </span>
                    );
                  })()}
                  {kcarDetail?.dates?.pfmncDate ? (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 font-bold font-mono" title="법정 성능점검일자">
                      📋 점검: {kcarDetail.checkDate && kcarDetail.checkDate !== '-' ? kcarDetail.checkDate : kcarDetail.dates.pfmncDate.replace(/(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})/, '$1-$2-$3')}
                      {typeof kcarDetail.holdingDays === 'number' && !isNaN(kcarDetail.holdingDays) && kcarDetail.holdingDays >= 0 ? ` (${kcarDetail.holdingDays}일 전)` : ''}
                    </span>
                  ) : null}
                  {(() => {
                    const accType = kcarDetail?.accident?.accidentType || selectedKcar.accident || '';
                    if (accType === '무사고' || accType.includes('완전무사고')) {
                      return (
                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 font-bold border border-emerald-500/40">
                          🟢 완전무사고
                        </span>
                      );
                    }
                    if (accType === '사고' || (accType.includes('사고') && !accType.includes('무사고'))) {
                      return (
                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 font-bold border border-rose-500/40">
                          🔴 사고
                        </span>
                      );
                    }
                    return (
                      <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 font-bold border border-amber-500/40">
                        🟡 단순수리
                      </span>
                    );
                  })()}
                </div>

                {/* 3. 사고 내역: 교환 건수·판금 건수가 있을 때만 한 줄씩 (둘 다 0이면 미렌더링) */}
                {(() => {
                  const chg = kcarDetail?.accident?.changeCount || 0;
                  const mtl = kcarDetail?.accident?.metalCount || 0;
                  const repNames = kcarDetail?.accident?.replaceNames || kcarDetail?.replaceNames || [];
                  const fixNames = kcarDetail?.accident?.repairNames || kcarDetail?.repairNames || [];
                  if (chg <= 0 && mtl <= 0 && repNames.length === 0 && fixNames.length === 0) return null;

                  return (
                    <div className="pt-1.5 pb-0.5 text-xs flex flex-col gap-1">
                      {(chg > 0 || repNames.length > 0) && (
                        <div className="text-rose-400 font-medium flex items-start gap-1">
                          <span className="font-bold shrink-0">🔴 교환(X):</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-rose-300">
                            {repNames.length > 0 ? `${repNames.join(', ')} (${repNames.length}건)` : `${chg}건`}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {(mtl > 0 || fixNames.length > 0) && (
                        <div className="text-amber-400 font-medium flex items-start gap-1">
                          <span className="font-bold shrink-0">🟡 판금/용접(W):</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-amber-300">
                            {fixNames.length > 0 ? `${fixNames.join(', ')} (${fixNames.length}건)` : `${mtl}건`}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })()}

                {/* 4. Large Price Display */}
                <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5 pt-1 pb-0.5">
                  <span className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-amber-400 font-mono tracking-tight">
                    {(kcarDetail?.price || selectedKcar.price || 0).toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-300">만원</span>
                </div>

                {/* 5. 옵션: 엔카와 1:1 완전 동일 옵션 매칭 및 설명 툴팁 적용 */}
                {(() => {
                  if (isKcarDetailLoading) {
                    return (
                      <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0c0e14] rounded-lg border border-[#232634] text-xs text-amber-400 font-mono animate-pulse">
                        ⚡ 옵션 정보 수신 중...
                      </div>
                    );
                  }

                  const rawOptList = kcarDetail?.options?.items || [];

                  if (rawOptList.length === 0) {
                    return (
                      <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0c0e14] rounded-lg border border-[#232634] text-xs text-zinc-500 italic">
                        추가옵션 없음 또는 기본 트림 사양
                      </div>
                    );
                  }

                  const targetOpts = splitOptionString(optionsTag || '');
                  const currentCompCat = kcarCompCatalog || compCatalog;

                  const exactOpts: { name: string; tooltip: string; detail: any }[] = [];
                  const fuzzyOpts: { name: string; tooltip: string; detail: any }[] = [];
                  const extraOpts: { name: string; tooltip: string; detail: any }[] = [];

                  for (const o of rawOptList) {
                    // HTML 특수문자 디코딩 (&amp; -> & 등)
                    const cleanName = (o.name || '')
                      .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
                      .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
                      .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
                      .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
                      .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
                      .trim();
                    if (!cleanName) continue;

                    const detail = getOptionMatchDetail(cleanName, targetOpts, 'comp_vs_target', targetCatalog, currentCompCat);
                    if (detail.confidence === 'exact') {
                      exactOpts.push({ name: cleanName, tooltip: detail.tooltipText, detail });
                    } else if (detail.confidence === 'fuzzy') {
                      fuzzyOpts.push({ name: cleanName, tooltip: detail.tooltipText, detail });
                    } else {
                      extraOpts.push({ name: cleanName, tooltip: detail.tooltipText, detail });
                    }
                  }

                  // 공식 일치(하늘색 ✓) -> 퍼지 추정(주황색 ~) -> 비교차 전용(오렌지 +) 순 정렬
                  const sortedOptItems = [
                    ...exactOpts,
                    ...fuzzyOpts,
                    ...extraOpts
                  ];

                  const displayOpts = sortedOptItems.slice(0, 8);
                  const overflowCount = sortedOptItems.length - 8;

                  return (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 p-2 bg-[#0c0e14] rounded-lg border border-[#232634]">
                      {displayOpts.map((item, idx) => {
                        if (item.detail.confidence === 'exact') {
                          return (
                            <span
                              key={idx}
                              title={item.tooltip}
                              className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-300 border border-sky-400 shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(14,165,233,0.2)] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition max-w-[210px]"
                            >
                              <span className="text-sky-400 font-black shrink-0">✓</span>
                              <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                            </span>
                          );
                        }
                        if (item.detail.confidence === 'fuzzy') {
                          return (
                            <span
                              key={idx}
                              title={item.tooltip}
                              className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/60 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition max-w-[210px]"
                            >
                              <span className="text-amber-400 font-black shrink-0">~</span>
                              <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                            </span>
                          );
                        }
                        return (
                          <span
                            key={idx}
                            title={item.tooltip}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400 border border-orange-500/60 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition max-w-[210px]"
                          >
                            <span className="text-orange-400 font-black shrink-0">+</span>
                            <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                          </span>
                        );
                      })}
                      {overflowCount > 0 && (
                        <span
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium bg-[#1e222d] text-slate-400 border border-[#2e384d] cursor-help hover:brightness-110 transition"
                          title={`추가 옵션:\n${sortedOptItems.slice(8).map(o => `- ${o.name}`).join('\n')}`}
                        >
                          +{overflowCount}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })()}

                {/* 6. 2D Car Damage Diagram Component (엔카와 완전 동일 양식) */}
                <CarDamageDiagram2D
                  selectedEncar={{
                    ...kcarDetail,
                    accidentType: kcarDetail?.accident?.accidentType || selectedKcar.accident || '',
                    replaces: kcarDetail?.accident?.replaceNames || kcarDetail?.replaceNames || [],
                    repairs: kcarDetail?.accident?.repairNames || kcarDetail?.repairNames || [],
                    replaceNames: kcarDetail?.accident?.replaceNames || kcarDetail?.replaceNames || [],
                    repairNames: kcarDetail?.accident?.repairNames || kcarDetail?.repairNames || [],
                  }}
                />
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 관심 차량 추적 관리 모달 */}
      <TrackedCarsModal
        isOpen={isTrackedModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsTrackedModalOpen(false)}
        onListChanged={fetchTrackedCarIds}
      />
    </div>
  );
};
```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { BiddingCockpitTabProps } from './BiddingCockpit/types';
import { useBiddingCockpit } from './BiddingCockpit/hooks/useBiddingCockpit';
import { CockpitSidebar } from './BiddingCockpit/Sections/CockpitSidebar';

// 외부 호환성을 위한 타입 Re-export

  BiddingCockpitTabProps,
  EncarItem,
  EncarSoldItem,
  EncarSoldStats,
  HeydealerBidItem,
} from './BiddingCockpit/types';

export const BiddingCockpitTab: React.FC<BiddingCockpitTabProps> = (props) => {
  const cockpit = useBiddingCockpit(props);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-5 items-start w-full">
      {/* ========================================================
          [좌측 고정 사이드바] 헤이딜러 URL/차량번호 입력 & 파라미터 (고대비 & 확대)
          ======================================================== */}
      <CockpitSidebar
        heydealerUrl={cockpit.heydealerUrl}
        setHeydealerUrl={cockpit.setHeydealerUrl}

        handleHeydealerAiEstimate={cockpit.handleHeydealerAiEstimate}
        setIsQuickPasteOpen={cockpit.setIsQuickPasteOpen}
        carNumber={cockpit.carNumber}
        setCarNumber={cockpit.setCarNumber}
        isSearchingCar={cockpit.isSearchingCar}
        handleLookupCarNumber={cockpit.handleLookupCarNumber}
        aiEstimateStep={cockpit.aiEstimateStep}
        searchStatus={cockpit.searchStatus}
        setSearchStatus={cockpit.setSearchStatus}
        manufacturer={cockpit.manufacturer}
        setManufacturer={cockpit.setManufacturer}
        carName={cockpit.carName}
        setCarName={cockpit.setCarName}
        detailModel={cockpit.detailModel}
        setDetailModel={cockpit.setDetailModel}
        yearModel={cockpit.yearModel}
        setYearModel={cockpit.setYearModel}
        mileageKm={cockpit.mileageKm}
        setMileageKm={cockpit.setMileageKm}
        optionsTag={cockpit.optionsTag}
        selectedEncar={cockpit.selectedEncar}
        expectedSellPrice={cockpit.expectedSellPrice}
        setExpectedSellPrice={cockpit.setExpectedSellPrice}
        outerRepairCount={cockpit.outerRepairCount}
        setOuterRepairCount={cockpit.setOuterRepairCount}
        auctionType={cockpit.auctionType}
        setAuctionType={cockpit.setAuctionType}
        targetMargin={cockpit.targetMargin}
        setTargetMargin={cockpit.setTargetMargin}
        userBid={cockpit.userBid}
        setUserBid={cockpit.setUserBid}
        safeBidCeiling={cockpit.safeBidCeiling}
        actualMargin={cockpit.actualMargin}
        actualPurchaseFee={cockpit.actualPurchaseFee}
        repairCostTotal={cockpit.repairCostTotal}
        memo={cockpit.memo}
        setMemo={cockpit.setMemo}
        savedSuccess={cockpit.savedSuccess}
        handleSaveToLedger={cockpit.handleSaveToLedger}
        targetCatalog={cockpit.targetNewCarCatalog}
        targetOptions={cockpit.targetOptions}
        unclassifiedBalance={cockpit.unclassifiedBalance}
        historyOptionTotalMan={cockpit.targetCarHistory?.totalOptionPrice ?? 0}
      />

      {/* ========================================================
          [우측 메인 워크플로우 4대 시퀀스]
          1. 자사 팔린매물 및 닷컴 동급 팔린매물 & 핵심 결론
          2. 동급매물 (엔카 시세 & 2D 상태도)
          3. 가격-주행거리 산점도
          4. 헤딜 낙찰시세
          ======================================================== */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-4">

        {/* ========================================================
            [1단계 & 핵심 결론] AI 시세 밸류에이션 & 실시간 회전율 통합 제어 센터
            ======================================================== */}
        <ValuationSummarySection
          carName={cockpit.carName}
          carNumber={cockpit.carNumber}
          detailModel={cockpit.detailModel}
          manufacturer={cockpit.manufacturer}
          yearModel={cockpit.yearModel}
          mileageKm={cockpit.mileageKm}
          expectedSellPrice={cockpit.expectedSellPrice}
          effectiveSellPrice={cockpit.effectiveSellPrice}
          aiRetailPrice={cockpit.aiRetailPrice}
          aiSafeBidCeiling={cockpit.aiSafeBidCeiling}
          targetMargin={cockpit.targetMargin}
          repairCostTotal={cockpit.repairCostTotal}
          safeBidCeiling={cockpit.safeBidCeiling}
          optionsTag={cockpit.optionsTag}
          outerRepairCount={cockpit.outerRepairCount}
          biddingVerdict={cockpit.biddingVerdict}
          isEncarLoading={cockpit.isEncarLoading}
          encarSourceUrl={cockpit.encarSourceUrl}
          autoplusStats={cockpit.autoplusStats}
          currentAssessment={cockpit.currentAssessment}
          targetCarHistory={cockpit.targetCarHistory}
          liveMarketStats={cockpit.liveMarketStats}
          liveEncarSoldStats={cockpit.liveEncarSoldStats}
          encarStats={cockpit.encarStats}
          encarValuation={cockpit.encarValuation}
          encarYearStats={cockpit.encarYearStats}
          encarSoldList={cockpit.encarSoldList}
          matchedAutoplusList={cockpit.matchedAutoplusList}
          matchedAuctionList={cockpit.matchedAuctionList}
          encarList={cockpit.encarList}
          soldTabMode={cockpit.soldTabMode}
          setSoldTabMode={cockpit.setSoldTabMode}
          showTrimColumn={cockpit.showTrimColumn}
          onRescanEncar={() => cockpit.fetchEncarComparable({
            url: cockpit.encarSourceUrl,
            carName: cockpit.carName,
            detailModel: cockpit.detailModel,
            manufacturer: cockpit.manufacturer,
            year: cockpit.yearModel,
            mileage: cockpit.mileageKm,
          })}
          onOpenHistoryModal={() => cockpit.setShowCarHistoryModal(true)}
          kcarStats={cockpit.kcarStats}
          isKcarLoading={cockpit.isKcarLoading}
          onRescanKcar={() => cockpit.fetchKcarMarket({
            carName: cockpit.carName,
            detailModel: cockpit.detailModel,
            manufacturer: cockpit.manufacturer,
            year: cockpit.yearModel,
            mileage: cockpit.mileageKm,
          })}
        />

        {/* ========================================================
            [2단계] 시장 비교 매물 영역 (엔카 실시간 시세 리스트 & 비교 매물 2D 상태도)
            ======================================================== */}
        <ComparableEncarSection
          encarList={cockpit.encarList}
          encarTotalModelCount={cockpit.encarTotalModelCount}
          encarFilteredCount={cockpit.encarFilteredCount}
          encarFilterStats={cockpit.encarFilterStats}
          selectedEncarId={cockpit.selectedEncarId}
          selectedEncar={cockpit.selectedEncar}
          isEncarLoading={cockpit.isEncarLoading}
          isInspectionLoading={cockpit.isInspectionLoading}
          encarErrorMessage={cockpit.encarErrorMessage}
          encarSourceUrl={cockpit.encarSourceUrl}
          carName={cockpit.carName}
          carNumber={cockpit.carNumber}
          detailModel={cockpit.detailModel}
          yearModel={cockpit.yearModel}
          mileageKm={cockpit.mileageKm}
          optionsTag={cockpit.optionsTag}
          showTrimColumn={cockpit.showTrimColumn}
          onToggleTrimColumn={() => cockpit.setShowTrimColumn(!cockpit.showTrimColumn)}
          onSelectEncar={cockpit.handleSelectEncarCar}
          kcarStats={cockpit.kcarStats}
        />

        {/* ========================================================
            [3단계] 가격-주행거리 산점도 (Price-Mileage Scatter Plot)
            ======================================================== */}
        <ScatterPlotSection
          encarList={cockpit.encarList}
          mileageKm={cockpit.mileageKm}
          expectedSellPrice={cockpit.expectedSellPrice}
          effectiveSellPrice={cockpit.effectiveSellPrice}
          carName={cockpit.carName}
          selectedEncarId={cockpit.selectedEncarId}
          onSelectEncarCar={cockpit.handleSelectEncarCar}
        />

        {/* ========================================================
            [4단계] 헤딜 낙찰시세 (도매 실거래가 20대)
            ======================================================== */}
        <HeydealerBidsSection
          heydealerSummary={cockpit.heydealerSummary}
          availableHeydealerGrades={cockpit.availableHeydealerGrades}
          bestMatchHeydealerGrade={cockpit.bestMatchHeydealerGrade}
          heydealerGradeCounts={cockpit.heydealerGradeCounts}
          selectedHeydealerGradeFilter={cockpit.selectedHeydealerGradeFilter}
          setSelectedHeydealerGradeFilter={cockpit.setSelectedHeydealerGradeFilter}
          heydealerBids={cockpit.heydealerBids}
          filteredHeydealerBids={cockpit.filteredHeydealerBids}
          selectedHeydealerBidId={cockpit.selectedHeydealerBidId}
          setSelectedHeydealerBidId={cockpit.setSelectedHeydealerBidId}
          selectedHeydealerBid={cockpit.selectedHeydealerBid}
          mileageKm={cockpit.mileageKm}
        />

      </div>

      {/* Quick Text / Spec Paste Modal */}
      <QuickPasteModal
        isOpen={cockpit.isQuickPasteOpen}
        quickPasteText={cockpit.quickPasteText}
        setQuickPasteText={cockpit.setQuickPasteText}
        onClose={() => cockpit.setIsQuickPasteOpen(false)}
        onParse={cockpit.handleQuickParseText}
      />

      {/* CarDetailModal 연동 모달 */}
      <CarDetailModal
        car={cockpit.cockpitModalCar}
        onClose={() => cockpit.setCockpitModalCar(null)}
        onUpdate={(updatedCar) => {
          props.onSaveToLedger(updatedCar);
          cockpit.setCockpitModalCar(null);
          cockpit.setSavedSuccess(true);
          setTimeout(() => cockpit.setSavedSuccess(false), 2500);
        }}
        onDelete={() => cockpit.setCockpitModalCar(null)}
      />

      {/* 🌟 보험사고 & 카히스토리 대형 종합 리포트 모달 */}
      <CarHistoryDetailModal
        isOpen={cockpit.showCarHistoryModal}
        onClose={() => cockpit.setShowCarHistoryModal(false)}
        carNumber={cockpit.carNumber}
        carName={cockpit.carName}
        currentMileage={cockpit.mileageKm}
        data={cockpit.targetCarHistory}
      />

    </div>
  );
};
```

// FILE PATH: frontend\src\utils\carColor.ts (Lines: 212)

```typescript
export interface CarColorStyle {
  dot: string;
  textColor: string;
  bgColor: string;
  borderColor: string;
  displayName: string;
}

/**
 * 차량 색상 문자열을 판정하여 점(이모지), 글자색, 배경색, 테두리색을 반환하는 공용 유틸리티
 * 
 * [설계 원칙]
 * 1. 계열 순서: 은색, 쥐색, 차콜, 검정 4단계를 명암 순으로 명확히 구분
 *    - 은색: 밝은 은빛 배경 (#e2e8f0) + 진한 텍스트 (#0f172a)
 *    - 쥐색/회색: 중간 회색 배경 (#475569) + 밝은 텍스트 (#f8fafc)
 *    - 차콜/진회색: 딥 차콜 배경 (#1e293b) + 연한 텍스트 (#e2e8f0)
 *    - 검정: 순수 블랙 배경 (#050505) + 흰색 텍스트 (#ffffff)
 * 2. 대비 보장: 밝은 배경(흰색, 진주, 은색, 노랑, 베이지, 하늘)에는 반드시 어두운 글자,
 *              어두운 배경(검정, 차콜, 쥐색, 네이비, 와인, 갈색, 초록)에는 반드시 밝은 글자 적용
 * 3. 우선순위: 구체적인 단어(은회색, 다크그레이, 차콜 등)를 광범위한 단어(회색, 그레이)보다 먼저 검사
 */
export function getCarColorStyle(rawColor?: string): CarColorStyle {
  const trimmed = (rawColor || '').trim();
  
  // 1. 결측값 / 미확인 / 중립 기본값
  if (!trimmed || trimmed === '-' || trimmed === '색상' || trimmed === '미확인' || trimmed === '정보없음' || trimmed === '미기재') {
    return {
      dot: '⚫',
      textColor: '#94a3b8',
      bgColor: '#1f222e',
      borderColor: '#334155',
      displayName: trimmed || '색상'
    };
  }

  const c = trimmed.toLowerCase();

  // 2. 은색 계열 (회색보다 먼저 검사하여 뭉개짐 방지)
  if (c.includes('은회색') || c.includes('은색') || c.includes('실버') || c.includes('silver')) {
    return {
      dot: '💿',
      textColor: '#0f172a',
      bgColor: '#e2e8f0',
      borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 3. 차콜 / 다크그레이 / 진회색 (회색보다 먼저 검사, 검정보다 한 톤 밝고 쥐색보다 어두움)
  if (c.includes('차콜') || c.includes('charcoal') || c.includes('다크그레이') || c.includes('darkgray') || c.includes('dark gray') || c.includes('진회색') || c.includes('먹색')) {
    return {
      dot: '⬛',
      textColor: '#e2e8f0',
      bgColor: '#1e293b',
      borderColor: '#334155',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 4. 쥐색 / 회색 계열 (중간 회색 배경)
  if (c.includes('쥐색') || c.includes('회색') || c.includes('그레이') || c.includes('gray') || c.includes('grey') || c.includes('메탈') || c.includes('metal') || c.includes('티타늄') || c.includes('titanium')) {
    return {
      dot: '🩶',
      textColor: '#f8fafc',
      bgColor: '#475569',
      borderColor: '#64748b',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 5. 검정 계열 (순수 블랙)
  if (c.includes('검정') || c.includes('블랙') || c.includes('black') || c.includes('흑색')) {
    return {
      dot: '⚫',
      textColor: '#ffffff',
      bgColor: '#050505',
      borderColor: '#27272a',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 6. 진주 / 아이보리 / 펄 계열 (흰색보다 먼저 검사하여 따뜻한 크림톤 부여)
  if (c.includes('진주') || c.includes('펄') || c.includes('pearl') || c.includes('아이보리') || c.includes('ivory') || c.includes('크림') || c.includes('cream')) {
    return {
      dot: '🦪',
      textColor: '#1c1917',
      bgColor: '#fef3c7',
      borderColor: '#fde047',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 7. 흰색 계열 (순백 배경)
  if (c.includes('흰색') || c.includes('화이트') || c.includes('white') || c.includes('백색')) {
    return {
      dot: '⚪',
      textColor: '#09090b',
      bgColor: '#ffffff',
      borderColor: '#e4e4e7',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 8. 하늘빛 계열 (파랑보다 먼저 검사하여 밝은 배경 처리)
  if (c.includes('하늘') || c.includes('스카이') || c.includes('sky')) {
    return {
      dot: '🩵',
      textColor: '#0f172a',
      bgColor: '#bae6fd',
      borderColor: '#7dd3fc',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 9. 남색 / 네이비 계열 (파랑보다 어두운 미드나잇 네이비)
  if (c.includes('남색') || c.includes('네이비') || c.includes('navy') || c.includes('다크블루') || c.includes('dark blue')) {
    return {
      dot: '🔷',
      textColor: '#f8fafc',
      bgColor: '#0f172a',
      borderColor: '#1e3a8a',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 10. 파랑 계열 (중간 로얄 블루)
  if (c.includes('파랑') || c.includes('블루') || c.includes('blue') || c.includes('청색')) {
    return {
      dot: '🔵',
      textColor: '#ffffff',
      bgColor: '#1d4ed8',
      borderColor: '#3b82f6',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 11. 와인 / 자주 / 버건디 계열 (빨강보다 어두운 계열 먼저 검사)
  if (c.includes('와인') || c.includes('wine') || c.includes('자주') || c.includes('버건디') || c.includes('burgundy') || c.includes('보라') || c.includes('퍼플') || c.includes('purple')) {
    return {
      dot: '🍷',
      textColor: '#fce7f3',
      bgColor: '#701a75',
      borderColor: '#86198f',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 12. 빨강 / 주황 계열
  if (c.includes('빨강') || c.includes('레드') || c.includes('red') || c.includes('적색') || c.includes('주황') || c.includes('오렌지') || c.includes('orange')) {
    return {
      dot: '🔴',
      textColor: '#ffffff',
      bgColor: '#dc2626',
      borderColor: '#ef4444',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 13. 베이지 계열 (갈색보다 먼저 검사하여 밝은 배경 처리)
  if (c.includes('베이지') || c.includes('beige')) {
    return {
      dot: '🍦',
      textColor: '#451a03',
      bgColor: '#fef3c7',
      borderColor: '#f59e0b',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 14. 갈색 계열 (어두운 브라운/초코)
  if (c.includes('갈색') || c.includes('브라운') || c.includes('brown') || c.includes('초코') || c.includes('밤색')) {
    return {
      dot: '🟤',
      textColor: '#fef3c7',
      bgColor: '#78350f',
      borderColor: '#92400e',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 15. 초록 계열 (포레스트 그린/카키)
  if (c.includes('초록') || c.includes('그린') || c.includes('green') || c.includes('국방') || c.includes('카키') || c.includes('khaki') || c.includes('올리브') || c.includes('olive') || c.includes('연두')) {
    return {
      dot: '🟢',
      textColor: '#f0fdf4',
      bgColor: '#14532d',
      borderColor: '#22c55e',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 16. 노랑 / 골드 계열 (밝은 배경)
  if (c.includes('노랑') || c.includes('노란') || c.includes('옐로우') || c.includes('yellow') || c.includes('골드') || c.includes('gold') || c.includes('금색')) {
    return {
      dot: '🟡',
      textColor: '#713f12',
      bgColor: '#fde047',
      borderColor: '#eab308',
      displayName: trimmed
    };
  }

  // 17. 기타 미분류 색상 (중립 회색 스타일 유지)
  return {
    dot: '⚫',
    textColor: '#e2e8f0',
    bgColor: '#1f222e',
    borderColor: '#334155',
    displayName: trimmed
  };
}
```
